# 📍 Location: Acolyte Realm

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Acolyte Realm](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Acolyte_Realm)

| Acolyte Realm |  |

| Region: Bristle Woods |  |
| Rank requirement: | Legendary |
| Map piece: | Keeper's Candle |
| Required Power Type: | ForgottenArcaneShadow |
| Required Cheese: | RunicAncientRadioactive Blue |
| Number of Mice: | 13 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 4 March 2009 |
| Years ago in Bristle Woods, a MouseHunter by the name of Plankrun opened a passageway to another realm. The passage was intended to act as an elaborate one-way trap for Acolyte mice. However, recently, the Acolyte mice have had success in reversing the portal's direction and are attempting to re-enter the Gnawnia Kingdom. |  |

## Overview

Acolyte Realm is a hunting location in Bristle Woods region.
It requires the rank of Legendary and the Keeper's Candle to access.
It cannot be manually traveled to. Instead, the Hunter is automatically transferred here upon catching a Realm Ripper in Forbidden Grove.

The main goal in this location is to catch an Acolyte. To do so, the Hunter needs to use Runic with a Forgotten setup.

## Mice

Most mice found in Acolyte Realm are part of The Forgotten Mice group.

Radioactive Blue and Ancient Cheese can attract some mice, while Runic can attract the exclusive mice in this location.
Rancid Radioactive Blue and Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue have the same attraction pool as Radioactive Blue.

Forgotten power type is recommended when using Runic, while Arcane is used with Radioactive Blue and Ancient Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

We aren't in Gnawnia anymore!

Look sharp, brave MouseHunter, for in this tower in the mist dwells an evil mouse known as the Acolyte. Only Runic cheese, made from runes collected in the Forbidden Grove, can attract the Acolyte, but be warned: few traps are capable of doing any significant damage to it once it is caught.

But. . . there may be hope yet. Legend tells of an island located far off the eastern coast of Gnawnia where a trap capable of defeating an Acolyte was once constructed. The General Store in the Catacombs sells blueprints that show how to build a seaworthy ship, should you wish to seek out the builders.

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the final step to Capture the Acolyte Mouse by using Runic Cheese in the Acolyte Realm to catch the Acolyte Mouse.

Adds 1 to the Acolyte counter (to generate better loot, described below)
When catching Chrono:
Generates bonus loot, including Frozen Scroll, Divine Orb, Forgotten Charm and Shadow Charm
The quantity of loot depends on the Acolyte catch counter

# 📍 Location: Afterword Acres

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Afterword Acres](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Afterword_Acres)

| Afterword Acres |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Map piece: | Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination |
| Required Power Type: | Tactical |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseMetaphor ManchegoAllegory AnariSymbolic Sirene |
| Number of Mice: | 15 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 2 September 2025 |
| Fight the blight, save the Infinitree, and carve Poetic Planks! |  |

## Overview

Afterword Acres is a hunting location in the Folklore Forest region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Viceroy and the Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination to access Afterword Acres.

## Gameplay Mechanics

In Afterword Acres, a hunter is tasked with cleansing the blight off the area and gathering Poetic Plank which can be used in Epilogue Falls. With the help of 3 droids, a hunter can choose to Harvest Literary Log, Carve Literary Log into Poetic Plank, or Cleanse Blight. The hunter must have Literary Log in their inventory before the horn is sounded to carve them. Condensed Creativity will double log harvested and planks carved, but has no effect on blight cleansed.

The droids will get more productive as more blight is removed and better cheese is used; and the bonuses stack multiplicatively:

| Blight | Multiplier |
| --- | --- |
| 1000 to 900 | x1 |
| 899 to 600 | x2 |
| 599 to 80 | x4 |
| 79 to 0 | x8 |

| Cheese | Multiplier |
| --- | --- |
| Standard Cheese | x1 |
| Metaphor Manchego | x2 |
| Allegory Anari | x4 |
| Symbolic Sirene | x8 |

Attracting the Blight Bringers and Herbicidal Maniac will increase blight by 32 points irrespective of whether they are caught or missed. Only Rainbow Quill, received through the upgrades in Table of Contents, provides a chance to prevent the Blight from regrowing.

## Heads-Up Display

Top-Middle Portion

- Afterword Acres
- A quick travel button to travel between Afterword Acres, Epilogue Falls, and Conclusion Cliffs
- A '?' button that provides useful game-play information about Afterword Acres, Epilogue Falls, and Conclusion Cliffs
- A Bar indicating Blight level divided in Multiplier Zones and current level of Blight out of 1000. The Multiplier Zones are:
1x
2x
4x
8x

Left

- Hunter's inventory of Literary Logs
- Hunter's inventory of Poetic Planks
- Hunter's inventory of Idea Germs

Clicking on the small basket icon opens up the inventory box that shows the Following Items:

- Hunter's inventory of Hypothetical Halophyte
- Hunter's inventory of Conceptual Coral
- Hunter's inventory of Plot Hook
- Hunter's inventory of Pearl of Wisdom
- Hunter's inventory of Motivation Mallet
- Hunter's inventory of Mythical Rainbow Diamond

Right

- Hunter's inventory of Condensed Creativity; which can be used by clicking on the "Ignite" button
- Productivity multiplier per Droid with an info button next to it. On hovering over it, hunters can see the components of Blight Level and Cheese that are contributing to the total multiplier

Bottom

- Level 1: Option for Harvesting, Carving, and Cleansing per catch
Harvesting: The number next to the Harvesting Droid depicts how many droids are tasked with harvesting Literary Logs. Next to this, the number of logs harvested (per catch) is mentioned. This number is calculated as Number of Droids x Productivity Multiplier
Carving: The number next to the Carving Droid depicts how many droids are tasked with carving Literary Logs into Poetic Planks. Next to this, the number of planks carved (per catch) is mentioned. This number is calculated asNumber of Droids x Productivity Multiplier
Cleansing: The number next to the Cleansing Droid depicts how many droids are tasked with cleansing the Blight. Next to this, the amount of cleanse (per catch) is mentioned. This number is calculated asNumber of Droids x Productivity Multiplier

By default, all three droids are tasked with harvesting and the number of carving and cleansing droids can be increased or decreased by clicking - and + in the droid box.

- Level 2 : Cheese
Hunter's inventory of Standard Cheese; which can be changed by clicking on the arrow button on the HUD
Hunter's inventory of Metaphor Manchego and Metaphor Morsel
Hunter's inventory of Allegory Anari and Allegory Algae
Hunter's inventory of Symbolic Sirene and Symbolic Seashell

Hunters can arm and craft the various cheeses by hovering over the corresponding HUD components and picking which recipe they prefer to craft.

All mice found in Afterword Acres are part of Afterword Anti-Arborists group.

Only Tactical power types work against these mice.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Welcome to the Afterword Acres, hunter!

This vast, verdant land is home to the mighty and generous Infinitree. This remarkable tree has the amazing ability to produce lumber willingly and infinitely!

Unfortunately, this big tree is in huge trouble. A terrible blight is being spread across the land by an invasive species of terrifying, tree-like mice.

Fortunately, the King has supplied you with some of the most sophisticated droids ever created. These miraculous machines are capable of doing any task you assign them to so you can have some collecting Literary Logs from the Infinitree while others are carving those logs into Poetic Planks. You can even assign some to cleanse this terrible blight and the more blight you cleanse, the faster ALL of your droids will work!

Once you've collected some Poetic Planks, take them to the Epilogue Falls to embark upon the next part of your journey!

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Fight the Blight and Save the Infinitree adventure in Afterword Acres.

## Location-Specific Effects

Rainbow Quill: Provides a chance to prevent the Blight from regrowing. Stacks with the Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base

### Bases

- Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base
When hunter encounters a mouse that results the blight to grow back, the base provides a chance to prevent the blight to regrow. This stacks with the bonus provided by Rainbow Quill from Table of Contents
- Naughty List Printing Press Base or Folklore Printing Press Base
When the Hunter has at least 1 Prolific Printing Paper in their inventory, the base's stats are boosted to:
4,500 Power
35% Power Bonus
35% Attraction Bonus
57 Luck
Consumes 1 Prolific Printing Paper per hunt

### Charms

- Baitkeep Charm - When going on a hunt with Baitkeep Charms armed, there is a chance that the charm will not be consumed and Bait will be instead

# 📍 Location: Balack's Cove

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Balack's Cove](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Balack's_Cove)

| Required Cheeses: | Vanilla Stilton CheeseVengeful Vanilla Stilton Cheese |

| Lose Gold: | Yes |
| Lose Points: | Yes |

| Release Date: | 17 May 2010 |
| This foreboding sea cave has fierce, unpredictable tides that can easily wash unsuspecting Hunters out to sea. Those who seek the spirits of this darkened grotto should arm a Shadow, Arcane or Forgotten trap, and use Vanilla Stilton as bait. |  |

## Overview

Balack's Cove is a hunting location in Tribal Isles region.

It requires the rank of Knight and Balack's Lantern to access.

Mouse pool here is determined by Tide and cheese used.

Catching a Riptide Mouse transfers the Hunter to Jungle of Dread, unless an Anchor Charm variant is armed.

## Tide

In Balack's Cove, a Tide mechanic is in effect.

- A complete Tide Cycle lasts 18 hours and 40 minutes. Each cycle includes these phases:
The rising Low Tide lasts 8 hours.
The rising Medium Tide lasts 1 hour.
The rising High Tide lasts 20 minutes.
The ebbing High Tide lasts 20 minutes.
The ebbing Medium Tide lasts 1 hour.
The ebbing Low Tide lasts 8 hours.
- The available mice depend on Tide level:
During Low Tide: all mice except Riptide Mouse can be attracted.
During Medium Tide armed with Vanilla Stilton Cheese: all mice except Balack the Banished, Tidal Fisher and Riptide Mouse can be attracted.
During Medium Tide armed with Vengeful Vanilla Stilton Cheese: only Balack the Banished, Derr Lich, Elub Lich and Nerg Lich can be attracted.
During High Tide: only Riptide Mouse can be attracted.
- Catching a Riptide Mouse transfers the Hunter to Jungle of Dread.
Using an Anchor Charm variant can prevent this from happening.

## Heads-Up Display

In Balack's Cove, the HUD shows:

- The Tide bar reflecting current Tide status:
Tide level
Whether it is rising or ebbing
- The Hunter's inventory of:
Pinch of Annoyance
Raisins of Wrath
Bottled-Up Rage

## Mice

All mice found in Balack's Cove are part of Balack's Banished mouse group.

Forgotten, Arcane, and Shadow power types have varied effectiveness against these mice.
All other types are ineffective.

Derr Lich, Elub Lich, and Nerg Lich drop loot that is used to craft Vengeful Vanilla Stilton.
This cheese can attract Balack the Banished and also boost the attraction rate of the Elder Lich mice.

When using Vengeful Vanilla Stilton, Forgotten power type is recommended.

The Hunter should be aware of Riptide Mouse which can move them to Jungle of Dread.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

I'm jolly glad to be out of Dracano, fearless MouseHunter, but this place isn't much better... I can hear whispering all around, and I don't think it's the sound of the waves.

This is the cursed resting place of Balack the Banished, and his legion of lich slaves. Shadow and Arcane traps work here, but Forgotten traps garner the best results, especially in conflict with Balack. The evil lord's followers respond only to the smell of rare vanilla bean, so keep plenty of Vanilla Stilton on hand. If you're after Balack and his Elder Liches, however, you'll need something with a little more bite-- Vengeful Vanilla Stilton, a nasty cheese made from the rage of Balack's eternal slaves.

If a legion of undead mice isn't enough to spook you, there's also the tides. The mysterious Riptide Mouse pulls the tides in and out several times a day, so unlucky Hunters caught in the waves will get washed out onto the shores of the Jungle of Dread!

The tides have three states; Low, medium and high. Low tide lasts the longest and there are no Riptide mice present. Medium tide increases the presence of Balack and his Elder Lich followers. Riptide mice will only appear during High tide, and should you catch one without an Anchor Charm at the ready, you will be washed out into the Jungle of Dread!

This will be a difficult fight, even for an experienced MouseHunter, but I believe in you! Get in there and lift that curse!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete Defeat Balack the Banished adventure in Balack's Cove.

## Location-Specific Effects

### Weapons

- Nutcracker Nuisance Trap - has a chance to make Derr Lich, Elub Lich, and Nerg Lich drop additional Bottled-Up Rage, Pinch of Annoyance, and Raisins of Wrath respectively.

### Charms

- Anchor Charms - these Charms prevent getting transferred to Jungle of Dread upon catching a Riptide Mouse
Empowered Anchor Charm
Festive Anchor Charm
Golden Anchor Charm
Spiked Anchor Charm

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

The Bazaar is a noisy place full of merchants selling all sorts of whacky wares!

If you've come from the Furoma region, be sure to visit the Cartographer here. Cartographers are experts in repairing damaged maps which will in turn open new hunting grounds!

Beware of the Burglar mice. They run rampant here at the Bazaar and never hesitate to steal your gold! Try arming your trap with some lavish Gilded Cheese to attract and capture the elusive Master Burglar!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete the first step of Capture the Eclipse Mouse adventure in Bazaar.

# 📍 Location: Bountiful Beanstalk

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Bountiful Beanstalk](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Bountiful_Beanstalk)

| Bountiful Beanstalk |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Map piece: | Tale of the Bountiful Beanstalk |
| Required Power Type: | Physical |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseBeansterLavish BeansterLeaping Lavish BeansterRoyal Beanster |
| Number of Mice: | 34 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Release Date: | 9 May 2023 |
| Climb this massive, towering beanstalk to reach the impressive, lucrative, and dangerous Castle in the Clouds! |  |

## Overview

Bountiful Beanstalk is a hunting location in the Folklore Forest region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Viceroy and the Tale of the Bountiful Beanstalk to access.

Hunters start off by looting Magic Beans on the Beanstalk to purchase Beanster Cheese. After catching Vinneus Stalkhome and looting 1 Fabled Fertilizer, Hunters can plant a vine up the beanstalk to enter The Castle in the Clouds.
There are 3 floors within the Castle, the Dungeon Floor, the Ballroom Floor, and the Great Hall Floor, each of which contain rooms that provide different primary loots.
Hunters are able to primarily loot Magic Beans and Lavish Lapis Beans in the Dungeon Floor, Royal Ruby Beans and Golden Harp Strings in the Ballroom Floor, and Golden Goose Eggs in the Great Hall Floor.
Hunters are able to collect Fabled Fertilizers from multiple Vinneus Stalkhome catches, and Giants at the end of each Castle floor.

Golden Harp Strings, Magic Beans, Lavish Lapis Beans, and Royal Ruby Beans earned within the rooms can be used to purchase different type of cheese in Bountiful Beanstalk.
Fabled Fertilizers earned can be used to plant vines to the Dungeon Floor, Ballroom Floor, or Great Hall Floor.

## Gameplay Mechanics

The Bountiful Beanstalk has 2 sub-areas: the Beanstalk and the Castle in the Clouds.

### The Beanstalk

The Beanstalk is separated into endless zones which are each 20 hunts long.
There are 4 possible type of zones: Standard, Super, Extreme, or Ultimate.
The type of zone determines how many Magic Beans are looted per catch.
Using Condensed Creativity doubles the Magic Beans looted.

| Zone | Loot Multiplier | BurningCondensed Creativity |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Standard Zone | x1 | x2 |
| Super Zone | x2 | x4 |
| Extreme Zone | x4 | x8 |
| Ultimate Zone | x8 | x16 |

At the end of each zone (the 20th hunt), the Hunter encounters the Vinneus Stalkhome until it is defeated.
Defeating Vinneus Stalkhome will reward one Fabled Fertilizer and the Hunter will enter a new zone.

Using Fabled Fertilizer, the Hunter can plant a Vine at any point of time to enter a floor within the Castle in the Clouds.
Do note that this will erase any progress made within a zone in the Beanstalk.
When the Hunter returns from the Castle in the Clouds, they will be placed at the start of a new zone.
This new zone may or may not be the same one which the Hunter was in prior to planting the Vine.

### The Castle in the Clouds

#### Floors

There are 3 floors in the Castle in the Clouds, the Dungeon Floor, the Ballroom Floor, and the Great Hall Floor.
To enter any type of Floor, a Vine must be planted whilst hunting in the Beanstalk area.
The progress within a zone on the Beanstalk has no influence within the Castle in the Clouds.

Each floor consists of one or more rooms, with each room being 20 hunts long (barring any use of Leaping Lavish Beanster Cheese).
The current room can be seen in the center of the HUD.
The next room can be seen on the right-hand side of the HUD.
Rooms are characterised by their type and quality (the type and quality of any room is random):

- 6 different types (This affects the type of loot dropped by the mice):
Magic Bean
Lavish Lapis Bean
Royal Ruby Bean
Golden Harp String
Golden Goose Egg
Mystery - loot is any of the 5 listed above
- 4 different qualities (This affects the Loot Multiplier):
Standard
Super
Extreme
Ultimate

While hunting within any room, the Hunter builds up Noise as shown in the Noise Meter each hunt.
At the end of a room, if the Hunter has maxed out the Noise Meter, the Giant will awake and initiate a Giant Chase.

The Bristle Woods is a region released on 4 March 2009.

- Forbidden Grove

| A huge tower looms in the distance, stretching beyond the visible skyline. Completely isolated with no signs of entry except for a lone, translucent portal. Contained inside are extremely powerful mice, versed in the arts of clockwork time manipulation looking to find a way out! |  |

## Overview

The Bristle Woods Rift is located in the Rift Plane region requiring a minimum rank of Grand Duke/Duchess and the Rift Detector to enter.
The Rift Detector can be purchased at the Living Garden Cartographer for 120,000 gold and 5 Dol Essences.
Leaving the Acolyte Chamber or any other chamber will not reset a Hunter's progress.

### Chambers

There are total 12 Chambers in addition to Acolyte Chamber. When Hunters first enter the Bristle Woods Rift or when they return after capturing an Absolute Acolyte, they encounter the Entrance. They are also presented a single Portal for Gearworks chamber. Hunters can choose to hunt at the Entrance or enter Gearworks. Entering Gearworks will start the progress towards catching Absolute Acolyte.

Hunters collect loot from the mice to empty Chambers and progress through the location. Once the loot meter reaches zero, three Portals open up. However, if the hunter has completed Ingress Chamber, four Portals will open. New set of Portals can be opened early by catching Chamber Cleaver mouse, which is preset in certain chambers after a certain amount of loot has been looted. When the Chamber Cleaver is available to catch, a small mouse figure appears in the top-right corner of the loot meter in the HUD.

## Loot

Items affected by Quantum Quartz will duplicate an extra piece of that loot. This loot will also deplete the loot of a chamber, counting toward completing the chamber. 
Items unaffected by Quantum Quartz or not directly dropped from mice (such as Enerchi generated from using Enerchi Charm or Super Enerchi Charm and bonus Calcified Rift Mist from using Rift Super Vacuum Charm or Christmas Crystalabra Trap) will not be duplicated and will not count toward completing the chamber.

If Hunters use Rift Vacuum Charm or Rift Super Vacuum Charm, Calcified Rift Mist may be dropped as loot. This loot is affected by Quantum Quartz and will also deplete the loot of a chamber, counting toward completing the chamber.

Time Sand will not drop as loot until Hunters have acquired the Ancient Hourglass from the Chronomaster in the Timewarp Chamber.

The loot dropped by the mouse depends on the chamber in which it is captured.

## Entrance

Hunters encounter the Entrance when first arriving in the Bristle Woods Rift and when returned there after capturing an Absolute Acolyte in the Acolyte Chamber.

Hunters are presented with a single Portal and entering it will transport them to a Gearworks chamber.

### Mice

Because Hunters are moved to the Entrance upon capturing an Absolute Acolyte, Runic String Cheese will attract all mice here, but its use (and the use of Ancient String Cheese) is not recommended since it has necessary special attraction effects in other Chambers.

## Gearworks

The Gearworks is the first chamber that Hunters will encounter when selecting the single Portal available at the Entrance. This chamber will also appear randomly as Hunters attempt to locate the Acolyte Chamber. Mice here will mainly drop Tiny Sprockets as loot.

There are a total of 25 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Gearworks. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Gearworks will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

### Mice

Capturing a Chamber Cleaver will open a new set of Portals, allowing Hunters to leave the Gearworks before depleting the available Rift loot. Other mice will continue to drop Rift items until the loot meter is reduced to zero.

- At 21-25 loot: The Chamber is too cramped for the Chamber Cleaver Mouse to come around.
- At 20 and fewer loot: The Chamber Cleaver Mouse is available to catch with Runic String Cheese!

## Ancient Laboratory

The mice found in the Ancient Laboratory are the main source of Ancient String Cheese Potions.

There are a total of 35 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Ancient Laboratory. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Ancient Laboratory will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

### Mice

Capturing a Chamber Cleaver will open a new set of Portals, allowing Hunters to leave the Ancient Laboratory before depleting the available Rift loot. Other mice will continue to drop Rift items until the loot meter is reduced to zero.

- At 29-35 loot: The Chamber is too cramped for the Chamber Cleaver Mouse to come around.
- At 28 and fewer loot: The Chamber Cleaver Mouse is available to catch with Runic String Cheese!

## Ingress Chamber and Pursuer Mousoleum

These chambers can only be entered once by Hunters. Once completed, Hunters will not encounter these chambers again until the area is reset by capturing an Absolute Acolyte.

Ingress Chamber
The Ingress Chamber provides Hunters with the opportunity to obtained a fourth portal between every chamber and the possibility of preventing the Portal Pursuer Mouse from appearing in other chambers.
There are 25 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Ingress Chamber. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Ingress Chamber will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

Pursuer Mousoleum
The Pursuer Mousoleum provides Hunters with the opportunity to remove the Pursued Curse if they have failed to remove it while in the Ingress Chamber.
There are 10 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Pursuer Mousoleum. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Pursuer Mousoleum will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

### Fourth Portal Buff

Completing the Ingress Chamber will provide Hunters with a fourth Portal after fully looting a Chamber or capturing a Chamber Cleaver.

### Pursued Curse

Failing to capture 3 Portal Pursuer mice in the Ingress Chamber will cause that mouse to begin appearing in other Chambers.

### Removing the Pursued Curse

If Hunters fail to block the Pursued Curse in the Ingress Chamber, a portal for the Pursuer Mousoleum chamber may appear. 
Completing this chamber will remove the Pursued Curse.

## Guard Barracks and Security Chamber

These chambers can only be entered once by Hunters. Once completed, Hunters will not encounter these chambers again until the area is reset by capturing an Absolute Acolyte.

Guard Barracks
The Guard Barracks provides Hunters with the opportunity to remove Portal Paladin mice from every chamber and the possibility of preventing the loss of trap luck from their weapon and base.
There are 40 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Guard Barracks. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Guard Barracks will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

Security Chamber
The Security Chamber provides Hunters with the opportunity to remove the Unlucky Alarm Curse if they have failed to prevent it while in the Guard Barracks.
There are 10 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Security Chamber. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Security Chamber will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

### Paladin's Bane Buff

Completing the Guard Barracks will remove Portal Paladin mice from every chamber.

### Unlucky Alarm Curse

This curse will remove the trap luck from the Hunter's weapon and base.
The alarm in the Guard Barracks will be triggered if Hunters make six hunts (successful or not) without capturing a Portal Paladin Mouse. Until the alarm reaches the sixth level and is triggered, a capture of the Portal Paladin Mouse will reset it to zero. Once the alarm has been triggered, it cannot be reset to zero except by completing a Security Chamber.Arming Runic String Cheese increases the attraction rate of Portal Paladins, making it easier to reset the alarm when needed.

### Removing the Unlucky Alarm Curse

If Hunters fail to block the Unlucky Alarm Curse in the Guard Barracks, a portal for the Security Chamber may appear. 
Completing this chamber will remove the Unlucky Alarm Curse.

There are 35 pieces of Rift loot available from mice in the Frozen Alcove. Every Rift item dropped as loot by the mice in the Frozen Alcove will reduce the loot meter. When the loot meter reaches zero a new selection of Portals will become available and mice will stop dropping Rift items as loot.

Furnace Room
The Furnace Room provides Hunters with the opportunity to remove the Frozen Portals Curse if they have failed to prevent it while in the Frozen Alcove.

This curse will cause one portal to be frozen closed when moving between chambers. The frozen portal can be unfrozen using a Portal Rekindling Key.
Each hunt will drop the temperature in the Frozen Alcove by 2 degrees. If the temperature reaches -30 degrees before all the Rift loot has been cleared, this curse will be activated.

### Removing the Frozen Portals Curse

Completing this chamber will remove the Frozen Portals Curse.

## Acolyte Chamber

This chamber contains the Absolute Acolyte. Hunters must have Time Sand in their inventory before this chamber will appear. The chamber will appear more frequently with higher inventories of Time Sand.

Hunters must charge the Timesplit Obelisk by capturing mice. The Timesplit Obelisk gains +1% charge for each piece of Rift loot dropped in the chamber. This includes any bonus Rift loot from using Quantum Quartz and Rift loot directly dropped from mouse from using any Rift Charms. Each hunt (including Failure to Attract and Failure to Catch) consumes 1 Time Sand from the Hunter's inventory and adds one Time Sand to the Absolute Acolyte's inventory.
When the Timesplit Obelisk reaches a 100% charge the Absolute Acolyte will stop accumulating Time Sand and is released but cannot be captured. Each hunt (including Failure to Attract and Failure to Catch) will still consume 1 Time Sand from the Hunter while each catch will consume 1 Time Sand from the Absolute Acolyte.
When the Absolute Acolyte's Time Sand reaches zero it can be captured. Upon capturing an Absolute Acolyte, hunters are transported to the Entrance.
If the Hunter's Time Sand reaches zero, they will be removed to a Gearworks. Hunters must then gather more Time Sand before they will be able to find and enter this chamber. The following journal entry is generated:

Hunter can retreat from Acolyte Chamber by clicking the Retreat button. Timesplit Obelisk charge and Time Sand from the Hunter and the Absolute Acolyte are kept. Hunter will be removed to a Gearworks and have to find another Acolyte Chamber to return.

### Mice

The mice found in these chambers are attracted only to Runic String Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Capture the Absolute Acolyte adventure in the Bristle Woods Rift.

### Hunting Tips by Larry

The tower consists of an array of chambers separated by impenetrable walls. The only way to move throughout the tower is to pass through the various portals that present themselves within the chambers.

Each chamber is filled with precious loot that the mice themselves are trying to collect in order to fashion a way of escape. Once a chamber has been emptied of its loot, look for a portal to be opened by the mice looking to escape into another more prosperous room.

You will need to find the Ancient Lab and Runic Laboratory chambers to obtain powerful potions that can be used to turn your String Cheese into a more preferred flavour to attract the obscure and powerful mice within the many special chambers of this Rift.

Once you have accumulated some Runic String Cheese, you can attempt to enter the Timewarp Chamber and acquire an Ancient Hourglass. Once obtained, you can then collect valuable Time Sand which will allow you to find the Acolyte Chamber and challenge the fearsome mouse itself.

There is also a plethora of special chambers that offer difficult challenges and unique abilities as well as powerful curses that will hinder your progress. Many great treasures await, so good luck and happy hunting!

Just... try not to lose too much time...

# 📍 Location: Burroughs

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Burroughs](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Burroughs)

The Burroughs is a region released on 4 June 2008.
It is located to the north of Gnawnia.

There are five known locations in this region:

- Laboratory

- Mousoleum

- Town of Digby

- Bazaar

- Toxic Spill

# 📍 Location: Burroughs Rift

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Burroughs Rift](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Burroughs_Rift)

| Burroughs Rift |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Rift Plane |  |
| Minimum Rank: | Duke/Duchess |
| Travel Requirement: | Rift Detector |
| Recommended Power Type: | Rift |
| Required Cheeses: | String CheesesTerre RicottaPolluted ParmesanUndead String Emmental |

| Lose Gold: | Yes |
| Lose Points: | Yes |

| Release Date: | 9 September 2014 |
| Just when you thought you understood and could deal with the horrors of the Gnawnian Rift, a new rift opens up! Tendrils of mist curl along the ground, and the terrifying cries of powerful mice echo all around. Do you have what it takes to take it on? |  |

## Overview

Burroughs Rift is a hunting location in Rift Plane region.

It requires the minimum rank of Duke/Duchess and the Rift Detector to access.

Mouse attraction pool is affected by Mist level and cheese used.

The main goal of this location is to catch Menace of the Rift to obtain a Null Gauntlet.

## Mist Mechanic

In Burroughs Rift, a Mist Mechanic is in effect. The mist level is between 0 - 20.

The Hunter can choose whether or not misting.

- When misting, a Mist Canister is consumed per hunt, and the mist level increase by 1 per hunt.
- When not misting, the mist level decrease by 1 per hunt.

The Mist level determines the attraction pool:

- The Hunter is in No Mist Zone if the mist level is at 0.
Only Standard String Cheeses work here.
Mice here drop Mist Canister that can be used to manage mist level in other zones.
- The Hunter is in Yellow Zone if the mist level is at 1 - 5.
Standard String Cheeses can be used to attract mice that drop Terre Ricotta Potion.
Terre Ricotta Cheese brew by Terre Ricotta Potion can be used to attract mice that drop Polluted Parmesan Potion.
Polluted Parmesan Cheese brew by Polluted Parmesan Potion can also be used here.
- The Hunter is in Green Zone if the mist level is at 6 - 18.
Standard String Cheeses can be used to attract mice that drop Terre Ricotta Potion.
Terre Ricotta Cheese can be used to attract mice that drop Polluted Parmesan Potion.
Polluted Parmesan Cheese can also be used here.
- The Hunter is in Red Zone if the mist level is at 19 - 20.
Standard String Cheeses, Terre Ricotta Cheese, and Polluted Parmesan Cheese can be used to attract mice that drop Rift Circuitry.
Polluted Parmesan Cheese is required to attract Menace of the Rift which drops Null Gauntlet.

Certain changes in mist level and Mist Canister supply generate additional journal entries:

- Mist level increases from 0 to 1:

- Mist level increases from 17 to 18:

- Mist level increases from 17 to 18 with String Cheese armed:

- Mist level decreases from 1 to 0:

- Mist Canister runs out:

## Heads-Up Display

The HUD is divided into 3 smaller parts:

### Left

The HUD tracks the Hunter's inventory of:

- Standard String Cheeses
- Terre Ricotta Cheese and Terre Ricotta Potion
- Polluted Parmesan Cheese and Polluted Parmesan Potion

The Hunter can arm those cheese and brew those potion from the HUD.

### Middle

The HUD displays:

- The Hunter's inventory of Mist Canister
- A button to mist/unmist with a tooltip on what to expect when clicking the button:

### Right

The HUD displays an image of some mice in the attraction pool depends on the mist level and cheese used.

## Mice

All mice found in Burroughs Rift are part of the Burroughs Rift sub-group of Rift Walkers group, except for the Tomb Exhumer which is an Event Mouse.

These mice have Rift as their main power type weakness. Other power types can still be used but are less effective.

Mist level and cheese used affect mouse attraction pool and in turn affects loot drops. Undead String Emmental attracts the same mice regardless of mist level.

- Mice in No Mist Zone can drop Mist Canister.
- In Yellow Zone and Green Zone:
String Cheese attracts mice that can drop Terre Ricotta Potion to brew Terre Ricotta Cheese.
Terre Ricotta Cheese attracts mice that can drop Polluted Parmesan Potion to brew Polluted Parmesan Cheese.
- Mice in Red Zone can drop Rift Circuitry.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

I swear, it's not cold, but I did get a chill just now! This place is spooky!

First up, you'll want to arm some string cheese and collect Mist Canisters from local mice. Then, start opening them up to attract spookier, stronger mice. Pay close attention to your mist meter while you're hunting. Not enough mist and you won't attract any mice worth catching. Too much mist and you'll attract big bruisers that are too much to handle for now!

Hunt in the mist for a while and you'll start collecting Terre Ricotta potions. The cheese you create from these will allow you to hunt new and scarier mice. These new mice will occasionally drop Polluted Parmesan potions, which allow you to create the rarest and most dangerous cheese in this rift!

When using Polluted Parmesan, you'll want to hunt only in the deepest areas of the mist, and try to catch The Menace Of The Rift. He has a fantastic treasure - a Null Gauntlet - which allows you to equip Rift Vacuum Charms. These charms siphon tiny amounts of energy from rift mice and condense it into Calcified Rift Mist - the main ingredient in creating rift charms!

Finally, once you've collected your Null Gauntlet and created some powerful charms, it's time to go back into the depths of the mist. The mice there drop Rift Circuitry, which you can use to craft two new powerful Rift Power traps and turn the tables on the rift mice!

It's a long list and a tall order, hunter, and the mice here are more powerful than most, but I know you can do it, so have at it!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete the Defeat the Menace of the Rift adventure in Burroughs Rift.

## Location-Specific Effects

### Bases

- Mist Meter Regulator Base - consumes 2 Mist Canister per hunt to keep the Mist Meter locked when in Green Zone

### Charms

- Rift Vacuum Charm - adds Calcified Rift Mist to mouse loot pool
- Rift Super Vacuum Charm - adds Calcified Rift Mist to mouse loot pool and generates an additional Calcified Rift Mist when one is dropped as loot

## Overview

Calm Clearing is a hunting location in Whisker Woods region.
It requires the rank of Journeyman/Journeywoman and the Calm Clearing Map Piece to access.

The main goal here is to hunt for Cyclops for the Great Gnarled Tree map piece to progress further into the region.

## Mice

Most mice here are part of Forest Guild mouse group.
Tactical power type works best against these mice, but Physical, Shadow, Arcane, and Hydro can be used as well.

Most mice can be attracted with Standard Cheese, except for Cherry that require Cherry Cheese.

## Overview

Cantera Quarry is a hunting location in Queso Canyon region.
It requires the rank of Count/Countess and the Queso Dowsing Rod to access.

In this location, the Hunter can loot Nachore to upgrade the Pump as well as to purchase the map piece for Queso Geyser in the Cartographer.

## Heads-Up Display

- Pump level and Nachore inventory

## Mice

All mice in Cantera Quarry are part of Queso Canyoneers mouse group, Quarry Quarries sub-group.

Shadow is the only power type working against these mice.

Higher-tier queso attracts mice that drop more Nachore.

- Using Wild Tonic in Cantera Quarry doubles the amount of Nachore looted.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

HELLO THERE, HUNTERS... hunters... (hunters...)

ECHO... echo... (echo...)

Alright, enough of that.

Watch your step! This quarry runs deep down below the surface and hunters that have entered have yet to return.

The only thing that can distract these mice from their work excavating the quarry is delicious queso! The spicier the cheese, the further into the quarry the scent seems to penetrate, bringing more powerful mice up from the depths, looking to taste the mouthwateringly-spiced queso. The stronger the mouse, the deeper they've explored, and the more Nachore they'll have. Spicier queso will help you find more Nachore much faster!

Nachore is very valuable in Queso Canyon. Not only can you use it to upgrade your pump, but it can be used to purchase a variety of goods from the General Store. The King needs the extra Nachore to keep producing pumps for new hunters to the area, so keep on extracting it and trading it in for goods, even after your pump is fully upgraded!

Good luck and don't fall in!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete some steps of the Extinguish Inferna, The Engulfed adventure in Cantera Quarry.

Generates a random Queso Canyon-related item per catch

| This small island was once attached to the mainland before a mighty storm washed away a massive piece of land. Members of the three tribes were then trapped where they were forced to work together to build a means of travel back to the mainland. ## Overview

Cape Clawed is a hunting location in Tribal Isles region.
It requires the rank of Legendary and the Ocean Navigation Kit to access.

While it is not required to hunt in this location to progress further in the region, the Hunter can find loot to upgrade certain weapons here.

## Mice

The mice found in Cape Clawed are from a variety of mouse groups.

Most of them can be attracted with Standard Cheese, with some exceptions:

- Aged requires Crunchy to attract and Physical power type to catch.
It drops Rhino Horn to buy RhinoBot and Enraged RhinoBot.
- Elder requires Shell to attract and Hydro power type to catch.
It drops Ancient Spear to buy Ancient Spear Gun.
- Grandfather requires Gumbo to attract and Tactical power type to catch.
It drops Thorned Vine to buy Thorned Venus Mouse Trap.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Congratulations on crossing the Rodentia Ocean, MouseHunter!

The Tribal Isles region is home to three tribes which have a long history of conflict. The eldest mice of each tribe now meet here in an effort to restore peace to the region.

Visit the Cheese Shoppe here in Cape Clawed and I am sure Gouda will catch your attention. Its sharp flavor has long been a favorite of the mice indigenous to the Tribal Isles, only surpassed by SUPER|brie+.

Cape Clawed also has a Trapsmith and a General Store. In addition to standard cheese-making supplies like Salt, Curds and Whey, the General Store here also carries Coconut Milk, which is a vital ingredient in some local flavors of cheese. I hope you came with a good stash of gold because the General Store here also carries some mighty expensive sets of blueprints.

Phew, that was quite a lot of information if I do say so myself... and I do! But I haven't even mentioned what hunting there is to be done here in Cape Clawed.

The majority of mice here are rather weak and offer little reward for their capture. Cape Clawed is, however, a meeting spot for the tribal elders. Each of the elder mice here are attracted to a very specific cheese made from secret ingredients. To capture the elder mice, you will have to visit their respective villages to the East and hunt down the secret ingredients to their favorite cheeses.

Well, I suppose these are all the tips I have for you, MouseHunter. There is much to do here in the Tribal Isles, so take your time, plan your moves, and try to ignore the menacing roar coming from that volcano. . .

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete some steps of the following adventures in Cape Clawed:

- Defeat the Tribe Chieftains
- Defeat the Dreaded Horde
- Slay the Dragon Mouse
- Defeat Balack the Banished

## Location-Specific Effects

### Bases

- Tiki Base - has a bonus of 6 Luck
- Tribal Base - has a bonus of 6 Luck
- Tribal Kaboom Base - doubles the range of Delicious Stone, Savoury Vegetables, and Seashell dropped

# 📍 Location: Catacombs

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Catacombs](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Catacombs)

| Catacombs |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Bristle Woods |  |

| Travel Requirement: | Repaired Mousoleum Map Piece |
| Required Cheese: | Ancient CheeseRadioactive Blue CheeseUndead Emmental |
| Number of Mice: | 21 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Lose Gold: | Yes |
| Lose Points: | Yes |
| Lose Cheese: | Yes |
| Release Date: | 4 February 2009 |
| Deep below the cursed soil of the Mousoleum are underground caverns that stretch to the west where long-forgotten mice roam. These powerful mice are highly resistant to most types of damage and apparently have a weakness for Radioactive Blue cheese. The creatures of the Catacombs seem strangely organized, as if they are preparing for something. Who knows what sinister secrets a hunter may uncover here...? |  |

## Overview

The Catacombs are a location in the Bristle Woods region requiring a minimum rank of Legendary and the Repaired Mousoleum Map Piece to enter.
The Repaired Mousoleum Map Piece can be obtained by taking the Tattered Mousoleum Map Piece to the Cartographer in the Bazaar for repair.
Hunters must capture the Keeper Mouse to obtain the Keeper's Candle, which grants access to the Forbidden Grove.
Mice in the Catacombs may steal points, gold, or multiple pieces of cheese if not captured.

## Catacombs Mice

The mice in the Catacombs are a mix from the Indigenous, The Shadow Clan, and The Forgotten Mice groups.
The Grave Robber and Zombot Unipire mice are from the Event Mice group, but are weak to the same Power Types as the other mice found here.
Hunters should arm Shadow or Arcane weapons, depending on their bait selection, though strong Forgotten weapons may outperform them when hunting with Radioactive Blue Cheese.

### Cheese Preference and Loot

Radioactive Blue Cheese, Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese or Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese will attract all mice in the Catacombs, except the Grave Robber, Ravenous Zombie, and Zombot Unipire mice.
Ancient Cheese significantly reduces the mice population of the Catacombs to just The Forgotten Mice and the Vampire Mouse. Arcane weapons are strongly recommended when hunting with Ancient Cheese.
Moon Cheese or Crescent Cheese removes a number of mice from the population, including all The Forgotten Mice, except the Scavenger Mouse.
Undead Emmental or Undead String Emmental are the only bait that will attract the Grave Robber, Ravenous Zombie, and Zombot Unipire mice.

## Hunting Strategy

While in the Catacombs, Hunters must capture the Keeper Mouse to obtain the Keeper's Candle, which grants access to the Forbidden Grove.

The mice in the Catacombs are a mix from the Indigenous, The Shadow Clan, and The Forgotten Mice groups, though the Grave Robber and Zombot Unipire mice are from the Event Mice group, but are weak to the same Power Types as the other mice found here.
Hunters should arm Shadow or Arcane weapons, depending on their bait selection, though strong Forgotten weapons may outperform them when hunting with Radioactive Blue Cheese.

Radioactive Blue Cheese will attract all mice in the Catacombs, except the Grave Robber, Ravenous Zombie, and Zombot Unipire mice.
Ancient Cheese significantly reduces the mice population of the Catacombs, to just The Forgotten Mice and the Vampire Mouse. Arcane weapons are strongly recommended when hunting with Ancient Cheese
Moon Cheese removes a number of mice from the population, including all The Forgotten Mice, except the Scavenger Mouse.
Undead Emmental is the only cheese that will attract the Grave Robber, Ravenous Zombie, and Zombot Unipire mice.

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Zounds! And I thought the Mousoleum was creepy!

The rodent horrors that wander these catacombs have existed since before the founding of Gnawnia, and have been forgotten by the rest of the world. Hunting these Forgotten mice is incredibly challenging since they have few known weaknesses. You will need Radioactive Blue cheese to attract the mice of the Catacombs.

Forgotten mice can be caught using a Shadow trap, but Arcane traps work better. Visit the Trapsmith here to purchase the Obelisk of Slumber -- an Arcane trap with a minty fresh scent! The Trapsmith also stocks some even more powerful Arcane traps.

Located deep within the Catacombs is a hallway too dark to navigate. Hunters often speak of Keeper mice wandering down the corridor using a magical candle to light the path. Perhaps, if you were to obtain one of these candles, you could do the same.

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete steps of the Capture the Acolyte Mouse adventure in Catacombs.

- Rift Antiskele Charm - removes Skeleton from attraction pool

# 📍 Location: Cerulean Skyport

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Cerulean Skyport](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Cerulean_Skyport)

| Cerulean Skyport |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Hollow Heights |  |
| Rank requirement: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Map piece: | Cerulean Armillary Sphere |
| Required Power Type: | Law |
| Required Cheese: | Sky Pirate Swiss CheeseSky Raider Romano CheeseAurora Bocconcini Cheese |
| Number of Mice: | 115 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Release Date: | 7 July 2026 |
| This floating port town is the centre of aerial commerce in Gnawnia with trade goods flying in daily from all corners of the Kingdom. |  |

## Overview

Cerulean Skyport is a hunting location in the Hollow Heights region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Archduke/Archduchess and the Cerulean Armillary Sphere to access Cerulean Skyport.

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Standard Cheese

Arming Standard Cheese while on the Skydocks or while Trading with any trader will allot the hunter to collect Corsair's Curd to craft Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese.

### Skydocks

Hunting with Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese in the Skydocks will allow the Hunter to collect Dirigible Debris to start a trade with an Atmo Trader.

### Trading

When enough materials have been gathered, the Hunter can start trading with a trader. There are 3 types of traders coming back from nine different regions the Hunter can trade with:

| Type of Trader | Trading Requirements | Number of Hunts |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Atmospherium Gas Shipment | 20 Dirigible Debris | 10 Hunts |
| Unrefined Cloudstone Shipment | 30 Atmospherium Gas | 15 Hunts |
| Aurora Spice Shipment | 40 Unrefined Cloudstone | 20 Hunts |

| Tribal Isles | Fiery Warpath | Living Garden |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fort Rox | Queso Geyser | Zokor |
| Moussu Picchu | Floating Islands | Table of Contents |

One of each trader will always be available. However, the region they return from will change once the Hunter returns to the Skydock. Each regions have their own Raid Intel that the Hunter can start gathering.

### Intercepting a Raid

Once the Hunter have 50 Raid Intel from one region, they can start Intercepting Captain Crook's Raid. The Hunter must be in the Skydock to start Intercepting

Captain Crook has recruited help from Mice from the region, so the Hunter should arm the respective Power Type for the region they're Intercepting:

| Tribal Isles Physical, Tactical, Hydro | Fiery Warpath Physical | Living Garden Hydro |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fort Rox Shadow | Queso Geyser Draconic | Zokor Forgotten |
| Moussu Picchu Arcane | Floating Islands Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law | Table of Contents Forgotten |

## Heads-Up Display

## Mice

Mice found in Cerulean Skyport while not intercepting a raid are part of the Cerulean Sky Raiders group.

Only Law power types work against these mice.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Welcome to the Cerulean Skyport hunter, where the stocks are as high as the docks!

This place has always been busy but it's absolutely frantic around here since Captain Crook and the Sky Raiders showed up. Their rampant raiding has put a real damper on the trade business up here and its effects are being felt all over Gnawnia so the King needs you to stop these meddling marauders at once.

In order to take down Captain Crook you'll need to find out where she is planning to raid next so that you can intercept her fleet before they do any more harm.

Start trading with the various airships that dock here and barter for the resources you need to make Aurora Bocconcini Cheese. Hunting with that cheese will attract the biggest, meanest, and most knowledgeable Sky Raiders that are bound to be carrying some important information that will lead you to their boss.

Once you've collected enough intel and finally know where Captain Crook is going to strike next, you'll need to set sail on your own dirigible and intercept her fleet. Rumour has it that she has been recruiting help from mice all over the Kingdom so be prepared for some unexpected reinforcements!

Good luck, fly safe, and don't look down!

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Liberate the Cerulean Skyport from Captain Crook adventure in Cerulean Skyport.

## Overview

Claw Shot City is a hunting location in Varmint Valley region.
It requires the rank of Lord/Lady and the Calm Clearing Map Piece to access.

The mice found in Claw Shot City are determined by the Hunter's active Wanted Poster.

Only Law power type works against these mice.

The mouse attraction pool is determined by the Hunter's active Wanted Poster.

Ringleaders can only be attracted with their specific poster after all the Accomplices has been caught in that poster.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Howdy, y'all!

This here town is chock full of the roughest, rudest and most downright reckless mice I've ever encountered! It seems like none of the old traps can bring them to heel, and they're just running roughshod over all an' sundry!

Your trap will need to be a new kind of tough to bring Law and order to this burg - be sure to visit the Trapsmith and get yourself a new trap!

The local Marshall is run ragged protecting the good people of Claw Shot City, and is turning brave hunters into honorary Sheriffs so that they can help! He's issued a series of bounties for the worst of the Wild Bunch gangs, and I've got a stack of them right h- oh, dagnabit! That Bounty Hunter Mouse just stole off with them! Well, you'd better catch him and get them back! Bring down that Bounty Hunter, reclaim the Wanted Posters, and corner the lawless gangs that run Claw Shot City!

You'll need to rustle up the gang's crew first before you can smoke out the ringleader of each gang - it's a good thing you can bring a friend along as your Deputy to help track down these lawless mice!

If you're finding it tough riding, try out the Prospector's Charms for a helpful power boost, or take a gander at the Sheriff's Badge Charm in the Charm Marketplace to really stick it to that Bounty Hunter Mouse!!

You'll be rewarded Fool's Gold for completing each Wanted Poster, which is pretty valuable around these parts! You can use it to purchase a stronger Law power type trap at the Trapsmith.

Good luck, and good huntin', pardner! As for me, I'm gonna lay low in the local saloon... It's almost high noon, and those mice sure are hungry!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete the Track Claw Shot City's Most Wanted adventure in Claw Shot City.

# 📍 Location: Conclusion Cliffs

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Conclusion Cliffs](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Conclusion_Cliffs)

| Conclusion Cliffs |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Map piece: | Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination |
| Required Power Type: | Law |
| Required Cheese: | Metaphor ManchegoPlotting PaneerStoried SagaDeus Ex Maconnais |
| Number of Mice: | 45 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 13 January 2026 |
| Write the conclusion to the Mythweaver's story! |  |

## Overview

Conclusion Cliffs is a hunting location in the Folklore Forest region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Viceroy and the Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination to access Conclusion Cliffs.

## Gameplay Mechanics

In Conclusion Cliffs hunters are tasked with writing Epic Conclusion to the Mythweaver saga by catching the Ultimate Mythweaver. To start off, hunters can hunt in the location without writing any pages. Only three mice are available at this time.

The epic conclusion is written in two steps - first a hunter has to complete six Chapters and then the Postscript of that Chapter.

### Chapters

To write the first chapter, hunter has to click Start Writing button on the HUD. Then hunters get the options to either get a random genre and length, or pick a particular genre and length by spending 30 Motivation Mallets. The genres are Romance, Adventure, Comedy, Tragedy, Suspense, and Fantasy. However, Fantasy genre is not available all the time. To complete each chapter, the number of hunts is decided by length of the chapter as per the table below:

| Length | Number of Catches |
| --- | --- |
| Short | 10 |
| Medium | 20 |
| Long | 30 |

While catching mice, each successful catch fills the pages in the book based on the cheese used as per the following table:

| Cheese | Pages per Catch |
| --- | --- |
| Plotting Paneer | 25 |
| Storied Saga | 50 |
| Deus Ex Maconnais | 125 |

Using Condensed Creativity does not affect the number of pages written. To select the second or subsequent chapters, hunters get the option to pick a short, medium, or long one from random genres. These options can be re-rolled by spending 3 Motivation Mallets, and the exact same chapter will not show up twice in a row. Progress can only be made when a hunter catches a mouse; Failing to catch or attract will not make any progress through the chapter.

### Postscript

After completing six Chapters, the hunter reaches the Postscript phase. This phase is 10 hunts long by default but can be extended to a maximum of 13 hunts by spending 30 Motivation Mallets. Weavers of all the genres written in chapters can be attracted here. The Attraction Rate of each Weaver is determined by the percentage of pages written by the end of all six Chapters. Every time a Weaver is caught (except for Ultimate Mythweaver), hunter's Notoriety in that genre increases based on the cheese used, as per the table below, and the Notoriety in rest of the genres decreases by 1.

| Cheese | Notoriety per Catch |
| --- | --- |
| Plotting Paneer | 25 |
| Storied Saga | 50 |
| Deus Ex Maconnais | 125 |

Using Condensed Creativity does not affect the amount of notoriety increase or decrease. Failing to catch a mouse consumes a hunt in this phase; however, if the hunter unlocked Rainbow Quill in Table of Contents and/or have the Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base armed, then they stand a chance to not lose a hunt.

### Fantasy Genre and Ultimate Mythweaver

To attract Ultimate Mythweaver in Postscript, the hunter has to complete chapters with some pages in Fantasy genre. The higher the percentage of pages written in Fantasy, the higher would be the Attraction Rate of Ultimate Mythweaver. However, to write the Fantasy chapter, hunter has to unlock it by gaining at least 80 Notoriety in all 5 genres.

During Postscript catching a Ultimate Mythweaver does not end the run. Hunter gets 10 hunts by default and can extend it to 13. Catching Ultimate Mythweaver, however, decreases the notoriety in all genres by 20 and does not increase the notoriety in any genre. The cheese only influences the loot dropped by Ultimate Mythweaver and does not affect its attraction rate.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

This is it, hunter, the end of the Folklore Forest... I hope you're not afraid of heights!

The Mythweaver has chosen the edge of these cliffs to make his last stand so the King needs you to put an end to his story for good.

The mice here are some of the Mythweaver's finest and most formidable creations, pulled from all the great works of Gnawnian literature across a variety of genres.

You'll need to use Plotting Paneer Cheese (made from those Plot Hooks you found in the Epilogue Falls) to bring these literary characters to justice and write pages in each of their genres.

Once you've written all the chapters, you'll begin the Postscript where you'll have a chance to battle with various versions of the Mythweaver inspired by each genre.

Catching those Lore Weavers will get you noticed and earn you some of that coveted Notoriety that all writers crave. Earn enough Notoriety in each genre and you'll get noticed by the Ultimate Mythweaver himself and have a chance to write in the most powerful genre of all: Fantasy!

All of your hard work throughout the Folklore Forest has led you to this pivotal moment. The King is proud of all that you've accomplished but don't rest on your laurels, this story needs a conclusion!

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Conclude the Mythweaver's Story adventure in Conclusion Cliffs.

## Location-Specific Effects

Rainbow Quill: Provides a chance to not lose a Postscript hunt after failing to catch. Stacks with the Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base

### Bases

- Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base
When hunter fails to catch a mouse in Postscript phase, the base provides a chance to not lose a hunt. This stacks with the bonus provided by Rainbow Quill from Table of Contents
- Naughty List Printing Press Base or Folklore Printing Press Base
When the Hunter has at least 1 Prolific Printing Paper in their inventory, the base's stats are boosted to:
4,500 Power
35% Power Bonus
35% Attraction Bonus
57 Luck
Consumes 1 Prolific Printing Paper per hunt

### Charms

- Baitkeep Charm - When going on a hunt with Baitkeep Charms armed, there is a chance that the charm will not be consumed and Bait will be instead

# 📍 Location: Crystal Library

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Crystal Library](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Crystal_Library)

| Crystal Library |  |
| --- | --- |

| Required Cheese: | Standard Cheese |
| Number of Mice: | 13 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 9 August 2011 |
| A magical barrier originally protected this library from mouse invasion until recently. Bands of magic and mechanical mice have circumvented the library's security measures in an effort to destroy the research Zugzwang once worked so hard to document. |  |

## Overview

Crystal Library is a hunting location in Rodentia region.
It requires the rank of Lord/Lady and the Crystal Library Card to access.

This location introduces Library Assigment mechanic to the game.

## Library Assignment

In this location, an Assigment mechanic is made known.

- The Hunter can choose to pursue a certain research. Each research type may has its own requirements.
Each research can have one of the possible assignments.
Each assignment requires the Hunter to complete a set of tasks. A task is usually either one of two types:
Catching a type of mouse with certain condition (cheese, charm, or power type)
Loot a certain item in a certain location
- Once all the tasks are done, the assignment ends and the rewards are given automatically. Most of the assignments reward a certain Tome of Wisdom and some Library Points, which is required to start high-tier researches.
- The Hunter can only pursue 1 assignment at a time.
The Hunter can use the Hunter's Hammer to end the assignment prematurely. After that, they need to wait for 1 hour to start another assignment.

## Heads-Up Display

The HUD shows Library Assignment status, including:

- The Hunter's current Library Points
- A button that can be clicked to bring up the Assignment Interface

### Assignment Interface

On this interface, more assignment information is shown:

- The Hunter's current Library Points
- The Hunter's Inventory of Fusion Fondue
- The list of all researches, including the names, descriptions, and requirements.

## Mice

All mice in Crystal Library are part of the Zurreal's Breed group, except the Crown Collector, which is part of the Indigenous Mice group.

SUPER|brie+ is required to attract Tome Sprite.
Scholar Charm is required to attract Zurreal the Eternal.

Most mice here are weak to Physical and Tactical power types, with varied effectiveness.
Zurreal the Eternal requires Zurreal's Folly to catch.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

The Crystal Library is an ancient trove of knowledge passed down through generations of only the most honored and powerful wizards; it was last under the care of Zugzwang before he vanished.

In the absence of Zugzwang a powerful mouse known as Zurreal the Eternal has managed to infiltrate the library's defences where he has unleashed an army of mechanical and magical mice to destroy the knowledge the library holds.

Your task is to defend the library and conduct research to rebuild the knowledge that has been lost. To start your research, pick an envelope to receive an assignment. Each individual envelope may contain one of many similarly-themed assignments.

Completing assignments will earn you rewards related to where your assignment took you as well as Library Points to show you're ready to start more varied and challenging assignments!

Your research will no doubt take you far and wide and test your knowledge of hunting! With enough knowledge you may even be able to discover how to find the elusive Zurreal...

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete most steps of Capture Zurreal the Eternal adventure in Crystal Library.

## Location-Specific Effects

### Weapons

- Zurreal's Folly - the only weapon that works against Zurreal the Eternal

### Charms

- Scholar Charm - required to attract Zurreal the Eternal

# 📍 Location: Cursed City

## Overview

Cursed City is a hunting location in Sandtail Desert region.

Living Garden, Lost City, Sand Dunes, Twisted Garden, Cursed City, and Sand Crypts are 6 connected hunting locations, forming the Living Garden complex.

They require the minimum rank of Baron/Baroness and the Living Garden Key to access.

Cursed City can only be traveled to when the Hunter's map is in Twisted state.

### Normal World and Twisted World

At a time, among 6 hunting locations in the Living Garden complex, only 3 can be traveled to. This depends on whether the Hunter's map is in Normal or Twisted state:

- Normal state: Living Garden, Lost City, and Sand Dunes are accessible
- Twisted state: Twisted Garden, Cursed City, and Sand Crypts are accessible

The map state can be changed from Normal to Twisted by:

- Catching a Carmine the Apothecary in Living Garden

The map state can be changed from Twisted to Normal by either:

- Using any cheese other than Duskshade Camembert or Lunaria Camembert in Twisted Garden
- Using any cheese other than Graveblossom Camembert in Cursed City
- Using any cheese other than Graveblossom Camembert in Sand Crypts

### The Living Garden Sequence

In the Living Garden complex, the Hunter follows 6 main steps in sequence.Step 5a can be done in Cursed City.

| Step | Location | Cheese Used | Loot | To Obtain |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Living Garden | Standard Cheese | Dewthief Petal | Dewthief Camembert |
| 2a | Lost City | Dewthief Camembert | Dreamfluff Herbs | Duskshade Camembert |
| 2b | Sand Dunes | Dewthief Camembert | Duskshade Petal |  |
| 3 | Living Garden | Duskshade Camembert |  | Access to Twisted World |
| 4 | Twisted Garden | Duskshade Camembert | Graveblossom Petal | Graveblossom Camembert |
| 5a | Cursed City | Graveblossom Camembert | Plumepearl Herbs | Lunaria Camembert |
| 5b | Sand Crypts | Graveblossom Camembert | Lunaria Petal |  |
| 6 | Twisted Garden | Lunaria Camembert | Living Chest | Fel EssenceGur EssenceFresh Twisted Garden Soil |

## Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame

In Cursed City, the Curses mechanics are in effect:

- There are 3 Curses in this location: Fear, Darkness, and Mist
- Each curse status can be Active or Lifted.

- In Curses Lifted state, Essence Guardian Mouse and Dark Magi can be attracted. Dark Magi also requires Shattering Charm armed.

The Hunter can arm Safeguard Charm to avoid encountering the Corrupt Mouse.

## Heads-Up Display

The Hunter can access and track the following in the HUD:

- Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame
- Garden Petals and Herbs
- Garden Essences
- Book of Living Recipes

## Mice

All mice found in Cursed City are part of the Lost City Mice group.

Only Arcane power type is effective against the mice here.

Mice here requires Graveblossom Camembert to attract.
Essence Guardian Mouse can only be attracted in Curses Lifted state.
Dark Magi can only be attracted with Shattering Charm armed in Curses Lifted state.

Most mice here can drop Plumepearl Herbs which can be used to purchase Lunaria Camembert to use in Twisted Garden.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

You're going to need a lot of Graveblossom Camembert here. The mice in these crypts don't seem to like anything else! Plus, any other cheese just doesn't feel... appropriate here in this world.

This city is heavily cursed, with not one, not two, but three separate curses! You'll need to purchase and use three charms to dispel the curses here, one charm for each curse. And beware, should the Corrupt Mouse show, all three curses will be renewed!

The reward is more than worth it though, as with all three curses lifted, the Essence Guardian mouse can be attracted to your trap, and they carry Garden Essences more reliably than any other mouse here.

While you're here, be sure to collect Plumepearl Herbs so you can use it to create more advanced cheese to keep hunting with!

### Adventure Book

- The Hunter can complete the Obtain a Rift Detector adventure in Cursed City.
- The Hunter can complete some steps of the Defeat Twisted Carmine adventure in Cursed City.

## Location-Specific Effects

### Bases

- Aurora Base - has a chance to generate a random Garden Essence
- Curse Breaker Base - has 50% chance to change a curse status from Active to Lifted upon catching a mouse
- Desert Heater Base - consumes a Flameshard per hunt to generate a random Sandtail Desert item per mouse catch
- Hothouse Base - doubles the range of Plumepearl Herbs dropped by mice
- Living Base - doubles the range of Plumepearl Herbs dropped by mice

### Charms

- Bravery Charm - controls the Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame
- Clarity Charm - controls the Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame
- Shine Charm - controls the Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame
- Safeguard Charm - removes Corrupt Mouse from attraction pool
- Shattering Charm - adds Dark Magi and removes all mice except Corrupt Mouse from attraction pool when in Curses Lifted state

## Overview

Derr Dunes is a hunting location in Tribal Isles region.

It requires the rank of Legendary and the Ocean Navigation Kit to access.

The Hunter's goal in this location is to collect Red Pepper Seed to progress further into Jungle of Dread and Dracano.

## Mice

Most mice here are part of The Derr Tribe mouse group.

Standard Cheese attracts mice that drop Delicious Stone, which can be used to craft Crunchy to attract mice that drop Red Pepper Seed.

Physical power type is very effective against mice here.
Other types should not be used.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Great Scott! What in the King's name are we doing here?

Members of the Derr tribe are ruthless, power-hungry war mongers. They topple even the most powerful of traps with ease, crippling hunters' courage and pillaging their gold. Hunting here is not for the faint of heart, but capturing a member of the Derr tribe does come with a handsome reward of gold.

You will need a powerful Physical Trap to hunt for Derr mice. If you are familiar with the Burroughs Region, perhaps you have discovered the secret research facility called Digby. The Trapsmith in Digby sells the powerful Digby DrillBot, which is a vital component in a strong Physical trap setup.

The mice of Derr trust no one, a trait that has made them very hard to trick into a trap. You'll find Gouda will attract a member of Derr towards a trap, but SUPER|brie+ is even more effective! The magical sparkle of SUPER|brie+ prevents mice from stealing more than a single piece at a time, a property that comes in handy here in the Derr Dunes, as the mice here tend to steal large amounts of cheese.

I’m heading back to town. . .this heavy suit of armor is quite cumbersome when navigating rocky terrain. Best of luck!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete steps of the following adventure in this location:

- Defeat the Tribe Chieftains
- Defeat the Dreaded Horde
- Slay the Dragon Mouse

## Location-Specific Effects

### Weapon

- Isle Idol Trap - doubles the range of Delicious Stone dropped

### Base

- Tiki Base - has a bonus of 6 luck
- Tribal Kaboom Base - doubles the range of Delicious Stone and Red Pepper Seed dropped
- Vegetation Base - generates a Spicy Red Pepper for each Red Pepper Seed dropped

### Charm

- Derr Power Charm - has a bonus of 600 power and 5% power bonus

| The ground floor of the Furoma Pagoda is where students most frequently train. Be cautious of your trap setup when hunting here, students are resistant to basic attacks. |  |

## Overview

The Dojo is a location in the Furoma region requiring a minimum rank of Grandmaster and the Stitched Furoma Map Piece to enter.
The Stitched Furoma Map Piece can only be obtained by taking the Shredded Furoma Map Piece found in the Mousoleum to the Cartographer in the Bazaar for repair.
Mice in this location may steal points, gold, or multiple pieces of cheese if not captured.

Hunters must capture the 3 Student mice found in the Dojo in order to obtain the ingredients necessary for crafting the cheese required to attract the Master mice found in the Meditation Room location.

## Dojo Mice

The mice found in the Dojo are a mix of mice from the Indigenous and Followers of Furoma groups.
The Followers of Furoma are weak only to Tactical and Physical weapons. Other Power Types are not recommended for use in this location.

### Cheese Preference and Loot

Standard Cheeses will attract all mice in the Dojo, except the Worker Mouse.
Maki and SUPER|brie+ will attract all mice in the Dojo, except the Hapless Mouse.
Maki Cheese has an attraction rate for the 3 Student mice of about twice that of SUPER|brie+ and four times that of Standard Cheeses

### Mice with Special Requirements

- Relic Hunter Mouse – This mouse visits the Dojo on an irregular basis. It does not consume cheese or charms. Hunters must have an Ancient Relic in their inventory to attract this mouse. The Ancient Relic will be taken by the Relic Hunter Mouse in exchange for a Relic Hunter Scroll Case.
- M400 – This mouse will sometimes be available in the Dojo when Hunters are actively working an M400 Hunting Research Assignment. It is attracted only to Fusion Fondue which will not attract any other mice. It drops a wide variety of loot.

## Hunting Strategy

Tactical weapons are generally more effective than Physical weapons in catching mice found in the Dojo. All other power types are ineffective against all but the Diamond Mouse and Gold Mouse.

Hunters must capture the 3 Student mice in the Dojo in order to acquire the materials required for crafting cheese necessary to attract the 3 Master mice in the Meditation Room.

Standard Cheeses will attract all mice in the Dojo, except the Worker Mouse, though it's attraction rate for the 3 Student mice is low. 
SUPER|brie+ will double the attraction of the 3Student mice over Standard Cheeses and will attract the Worker Mouse, but not the Hapless Mouse.
Maki Cheese will double the attraction of the 3Student mice over SUPER|brie+ and will attract the Worker Mouse, but not the Hapless Mouse.

### Location-Specific Effects

#### Bases

- The limited edition Furoma Base has a chance of looting an extra Student Token in the Dojo upon capturing one of the Student mice.

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete steps to Capture the Master of the Dojo by gathering tokens from student mice in the Dojo.

### Library Assignments

- M400 Bait Research Assignment - This assignment may require Hunters to track down specific items from Dojo mice in order to complete it.
- M400 Hunting Research Assignment - This assignment may require capturing the M400 in the Dojo.

### Tournaments

Dojo mice are worth points during some Tournaments.

### Treasure Maps

Many mice in the Dojo are often found on Relic Hunter Treasure Maps.

### Hunting tips by Larry

Welcome to the Dojo, where students of the three Furoma schools train. Catch one of these students, and there is a very good chance they will drop one of their school's tokens, which are vital in the creation of three very special forms of cheese. Read the descriptions of the tokens you collect for cheese-crafting clues. There are also rumors that one of Plankrun's Journal Pages can be found in the Dojo, which may hold some interesting information about cheese recipes.

One of the Furoma students' weaknesses is Maki cheese. You can make Maki by smashing three pieces of SUPER|brie+ with your Hunter’s Hammer then combining them with three Curds and Whey and one piece of Nori.

Confused about crafting or how to make cheese? You can read the research of your fellow hunters in the MouseHunt Wiki for more help.

Once you've collected tokens and made new types of cheese, you'll want to head to the Meditation Room where you can hunt the masters. I have a suspicion that the incredibly dangerous Assassin mice may be in the Dojo on a hunting mission of their own. Catching one these mice may reveal clues on better ways to combat the forces of Furoma.

## Overview

Dracano is a hunting location in Tribal Isles region.

It requires a minimum Rank of Knight and the Ocean Navigation Kit to access.

Inferno Havarti Cheese, which is required to attract mice here, can be crafted using various loot from Elub Shore, Nerg Plains, Derr Dunes, and Jungle of Dread.

The main goal in this location is to catch Dragon Mouse to loot a Dragon's Chest which can be opened to obtain the Zugzwang's Scarf.

## Mice

All mice found in Dracano are part of the Draconic Brood group.

Inferno Havarti Cheese is required to attract mice here.

Draconic power type is required to catch Dragon Mouse.
Draconic is also the primary weakness of Draconic Warden Mouse and Whelpling Mouse, while Physical, Tactical, and Hydro are less effective against those 2 mice.
