# 📍 Location: Draconic Depths

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Draconic Depths](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Draconic_Depths)

| Draconic Depths |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Map piece: | Tale of the First Dragons |
| Required Power Type: | Draconic |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseFiery FontinaIcy IsabirraPoisonous ProvoloneElemental Emmental |
| Number of Mice: | 30 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Release Date: | 13 August 2024 |
| Do you dare to descend into dark and dangerous depths where dread, despair, and desolation dominate a devastatingly dismal den of destruction? Beware, here be dragons! |  |

## Overview

Draconic Depths is a hunting location in the Folklore Forest region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Viceroy and the Tale of the First Dragons to access Draconic Depths.

## Gameplay Mechanics

Draconic Depths is divided in several sub-locations that do not need to be traveled to explicitly. The sub-locations are -

| Surface<br>Cavern<br>Flame Cavern<br>Ice Lair<br>Toxic Tunnel<br>Elemental Dragons' Den |
| --- |

### Surface

When hunters first enter Draconic Depths they hunt here to forge Fire Dowsing Rod, Ice Dowsing Rod, and/or Poison Dowsing Rod using three crucibles numbered from #1 to #3. Each one is of the element types - Fire, Ice, or Poison - corresponding to the similarly named mice groups. By spending 15 Dragon Embers, the crucibles can be individually rolled for another type. This rolling does not follow a predictable pattern and new crucible appearing would be of a random element. When a crucible is rolled, following message appears in the Hunter's Journal

I used 15 Dragon Embers to reroll Crucible #xx from <Element Name> to <Element Name>.

Every time a mouse is caught, crucible may progress up. When the progress reaches 20, crucible(s) is(are) forged completely and catching more mice from that mouse sub-group would not take the number higher. Failing to catch or attract a mouse does not impact the progress in any way and the progress in a crucible is not reset when it is rolled. Additionally, if hunters enter a cavern with partial progress on crucible, that progress will not be lost. Condensed Creativity doubles the progress per catch and the base rate of progress for dowsing rods is mentioned in the table below.

| Mouse | Sub-group | Crucible Progress |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Fire DowsingRod | Ice DowsingRod | Poison DowsingRod |  |  |
| Squire Sizzleton | Fire Dragons | 1 |  |  |
| Torchbearer Tinderhelm | Fire Dragons | 1 |  |  |
| Colonel Crisp | Fire Dragons | 1 |  |  |
| Frostnip Icebound | Ice Dragons |  | 1 |  |
| Blizzara Winterosa | Ice Dragons |  | 1 |  |
| Iciclesius the Defender | Ice Dragons |  | 1 |  |
| Goopus Dredgemore | Poison Dragons |  |  | 1 |
| Noxio Sludgewell | Poison Dragons |  |  | 1 |
| Dreck Grimehaven | Poison Dragons |  |  | 1 |
| Tranquilia Protecticus | Elemental Dragons | 10 | 10 | 10 |

### Cavern

A minimum of one dowsing rod has to be activated to enter Caverns. Activating more dowsing rods of same type does not change the attraction rates of mice. It, instead, unlocks higher loot drop multiplier. Forged dowsing rods can be selected in following ways

- If only one dowsing rod is selected of any element, hunter enters the Shallow Cavern and gets the base drop rate of loot
- If two dowsing rods of same element are selected, hunter enters the Deep Cavern and gets 2x {the base drop rate of loot}
- If three dowsing rods of same element are selected, hunter enters the Abyssal Cavern and gets 4x {the base drop rate of loot}
- If hunter selects two dowsing rods of same element and a third dowsing rod of different element, following message appears in the HUD:

No cavern found! Try a different combination.

After entering a cavern, hunters get 10 hunts before the Cavern would collapse; however, the caverns can be reinforced infinite number of times for a maximum of 25 hunts each time. The only way to exit a cavern is to hunt there till it collapses. Hunters get a Replicart here that has the ability to replicate following loot dropped by mice.

| Adorned Empyrean Jewel<br>Ancient Charm<br>Ancient Relic<br>Baitkeep Charm<br>Bottled Cold Fusion<br>Condensed Creativity<br>Diamond<br>Dragon Ember Potion<br>Dragonbane Charm<br>Dragonbane Potion<br>Dragonhide Sliver<br>Dragon's Breath Opal<br>Dragon's Eye<br>Elemental Emmental Cheese<br>Extreme Ancient Charm<br>Extreme Dragonbane Charm<br>Extreme Luck Charm<br>Extreme Power Charm<br>Extreme Spore Charm | Fire Grub<br>Flamin' Spice Leaf<br>Ful'mina's Charged Toothlet<br>Ful'Mina's Tooth<br>Ice Grub<br>Icuri Essence<br>Kalor'ignis Rib<br>Large Steel Barrel<br>Luck Charm<br>Magic Essence<br>Medium Steel Barrel<br>Mythical Dragon Heart<br>Poison Grub<br>Pollutinum<br>Power Charm<br>Radioactive Curd<br>Radioactive Sludge<br>Rainbow Charm<br>Rainbow Spore Charm | Small Steel Barrel<br>Solidified Amber Queso<br>Spore Charm<br>Storm Cell<br>Super Ancient Charm<br>Super Dragonbane Charm<br>Super Luck Charm<br>Super Power Charm<br>Super Spore Charm<br>Tungsten<br>Ultimate Ancient Charm<br>Ultimate Charm<br>Ultimate Dragonbane Charm<br>Ultimate Luck Charm<br>Ultimate Lucky Power Charm<br>Ultimate Power Charm<br>Ultimate Spore Charm<br>Unstable Magnum Opus<br>War Scrap |
| --- | --- | --- |

The total loot and type of bait determine which mice can be attracted in the caverns. The ranges for loot are 0-99, 100-749, and 750+. Using Condensed Creativity doubles Fire Grub, Ice Grub, Poison Grub, and Dragon's Breath Opal in loot from mice and in Replicart™. When the run ends and cavern collapses, hunters get a choice to Keep, Burn, or Incinerate the loot inside Replicart™ depending on the caverns. After making this decision, hunters return to the Surface.

#### Flame Cavern

While entering the Cavern, if majority of rods activated are Fire Dowsing Rods, then the hunter enters Flame Cavern. To reinforce this Cavern, hunters need to spend 15 Dragon Embers per hunt. Only Standard Cheese and Fiery Fontina can be used here to attract mice. When this cavern collapses, hunters get a choice to either Keep or Burn the loot inside Replicart™. If they choose to Keep it, they will instantly receive all the loot items inside and if they choose to Burn it, they will instantly receive a quantity of Dragon Embers equal to the total loot in the Replicart™.

#### Ice Lair

While entering the Cavern, if majority of rods activated are Ice Dowsing Rods, then the hunter enters Ice Lair. To reinforce this Lair, hunters need to spend 15 Dragon Embers per hunt. Only Standard Cheese and Icy Isabirra can be used here to attract mice. When this Lair collapses, hunters get a choice to either Keep or Burn the loot inside Replicart™. If they choose to Keep it, they will instantly receive all the loot items inside and if they choose to Burn it, they will instantly receive a quantity of Dragon Embers equal to the total loot in the Replicart™.

#### Toxic Tunnel

While entering the Cavern, if majority of rods activated are Poison Dowsing Rods, then the hunter enters Toxic Tunnel. To reinforce this Cavern, hunters need to spend 15 Dragon Embers per hunt. Only Standard Cheese and Poisonous Provolone can be used here to attract mice. When this Tunnel collapses, hunters get a choice to either Keep or Burn the loot inside Replicart™. If they choose to Keep it, they will instantly receive all the loot items inside and if they choose to Burn it, they will instantly receive a quantity of Dragon Embers equal to the total loot in the Replicart™.

#### Elemental Dragons' Den

While entering the Cavern, if one dowsing rod of each element is activated, the hunter enters Elemental Dragons' Den. To reinforce this Cavern, hunters need to spend 75 Dragon Embers per hunt. Any bait among Standard Cheese, Fiery Fontina, Icy Isabirra, Poisonous Provolone, and Elemental Emmental can be used here to attract mice. When this Den collapses, hunters get a choice to Keep, Burn, or Incinerate the loot inside Replicart™. If they choose to Keep it, they will instantly receive all the loot items inside; if they choose to Burn it, they will instantly receive a quantity of Dragon Embers equal to the total loot in the Replicart™; and if they choose to Incinerate it, they will instantly receive a quantity of Dragon Ashes equal to the total loot in the Replicart™.

## Heads-Up Display

The HUD always shows the following information:

Top-Middle Portion

- Name of the Zone
- A '?' button that provides useful game-play information about Crucibles at the Surface, and Caverns, Replicart™, and Keeping or Burning loot in Replicart™

Left

- Hunter's inventory of Dragon Ember
- Hunter's inventory of Dragon Ashes
- Hunter's inventory of Dragon's Breath Opal
- Hunter's inventory of Dragonhide Sliver

Clicking on the small basket icon opens up the inventory box that shows the Following Items:

- Hunter's inventory of Dragon Ember Potion
- Hunter's inventory of Mythical Dragon Heart
- Hunter's inventory of Dragon's Eye

Right

- Hunter's inventory of Condensed Creativity; which can be used by clicking on the "Ignite" button

Bottom

- Hunter's choice to automatically arm or disarm their Standard Cheese after exiting a Cavern. That status can be toggled by clicking on it
- Hunter's inventory of Standard Cheese; which can be changed by clicking on the arrow button on the HUD
- Hunter's inventory of Fiery Fontina and Fire Grub
- Hunter's inventory of Icy Isabirra and Ice Grub
- Hunter's inventory of Poisonous Provolone and Poison Grub
- Hunter's inventory of Elemental Emmental

Hunters can arm and craft the various cheeses by hovering over the corresponding HUD components and picking which recipe they prefer to craft.

Following information is available on HUD only in specific scenarios

- While on the Surface:
Three Crucibles, their elemental types, their progress out of 20, and a button to roll the crucibles individually
When the Progress in Crucible is not complete, the following message is displayed below it: Forge Dowsing Rods to enter the Dragon Caverns
When the Progress in Crucible is complete,
Progress Bar says 'Ready' and on clicking, it says 'Selected'
Based on how many dowsing rods are selected, following message is displayed: Explore the <Cavern type>
On returning from a Cavern, if a hunter has not Claimed the Replicart™, the Replicart™ appears next to the third Crucible showing the amount of loot and a button to claim it. The message below it says Claim your Replicart™ before exploring the caverns!. This message appears even though the progress in crucibles is 0/20

- While in the Cavern:
The Replicart™ in the middle showing the amount of loot and a button to 'Peek Inside'
To the Right, Number of Hunts until the cavern collapses out of example: xx/25, and a button to 'Reinforce' the cavern to a maximum of 25 hunts
At the Bottom in middle, a "Loot Bar" shows how much loot has been collected in this cavern depicting three zones:
0-99
100-249
250-749
When the hunter has collected more than 749 loot, the Replicart icon stays at the right-most end on this bar

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

G... g... greetings, Hunter, I'm so glad you're here!

We've discovered this awful place with caverns and fissures leading to something terrifying deep below. I'm afraid this won't be another fairy tale like your last two adventures. This is something much older, and much, much more dangerous!

Our most ancient legends described a place deep underground where all of Gnawnia's dragons originated... but no one thought it was actually a real place! We don't know much about it but one thing we know for sure is that we are in deep trouble.

The King believes that the Mythweaver is down there right now trying to amass an army of dragons to lead against the Kingdom. So we need you to navigate these terrifying tunnels, seek him out, and put a stop to his heinous plan.

The caverns below are a dark and confusing maze so in order to find your way, you'll need to forge some Dowsing Rods up here on the surface to guide you.

The King's best artificers have designed and built the most advanced droids ever conceived for this very purpose. These marvelous machines are not only capable of forging the Dowsing Rods but they will also accompany you into the caverns and load additional loot into their incredible new Replicart™!

Your job is to fend off the dreadful dragons that will no doubt defend their hoard while you're down there. You'll only have a limited time before the caverns collapse in on you... unless you can find a way to reinforce them.

G... g... good luck, Hunter, you're going to need it!

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Vanquish the Mythical Dragon Emperor adventure in Draconic Depths.

## Overview

Elub Shore is a hunting location in Tribal Isles region.

It requires the rank of Legendary and the Ocean Navigation Kit to access.

The Hunter's goal in this location is to collect Blue Pepper Seed to progress further into Jungle of Dread and Dracano.

## Mice

Most mice here are part of The Elub Tribe mouse group.

Standard Cheese attracts mice that drop Seashell, which can be used to craft Shell to attract mice that drop Blue Pepper Seed.

Hydro power type is very effective against mice here.
Other types should not be used.

| Elub Chieftain | 16,560 | 7,520 |  | X | Ancient Frayed Blueprint PieceBlue Pepper Seed |
| Mystic | 7,500 | 2,000 | X |  | Seashell |
| Pack | 6,000 | 908 | X |  | Seashell |

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

I welcome you to Elub Shore, although the mice of the Elub Tribe do not share my sense of hospitality!

The mice of the Elub Tribe treasure wisdom and knowledge over strength and power. As a result, you'll find the Shore an excellent place to expand your hunting knowledge and move up in the ranks of MouseHunting! To be successful here, you will need either Gouda or SUPER|brie+ and a Hydro weapon. If you lack a Hydro weapon, you can acquire one from the Trapsmith here!

You may notice that some of the mice here carry Seashells. Mice of the Elub Tribe enjoy the salt deposits found in the shells, and grind them up to make an oddly shaped delicacy called Shell Cheese. If you figure out the recipe, be sure you are well prepared, as I suspect the most skilled mice in the tribe, including their leader, the Chieftain, will quickly attack your trap.

Oh, and one more thing... Do you mind turning down the volume on that Hunter's Horn? I am going to find a comfy spot on the beach to relax!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete steps of the following adventure in this location:

- Defeat the Tribe Chieftains
- Defeat the Dreaded Horde
- Slay the Dragon Mouse

- Tribal Kaboom Base - doubles the range of Seashell and Blue Pepper Seed dropped
- Vegetation Base - generates a Magical Blue Pepper for each Blue Pepper Seed dropped

### Charm

- Elub Power Charm - has a bonus of 600 power and 5% power bonus

# 📍 Location: Epilogue Falls

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Epilogue Falls](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Epilogue_Falls)

| Epilogue Falls |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Map piece: | Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination |
| Required Power Type: | Hydro |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseMetaphor ManchegoAllegory AnariSymbolic Sirene |
| Number of Mice: | 15 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 2 September 2025 |
| Build a barrel, ride the rapids, and journey into the falls! |  |

## Overview

Epilogue Falls is a hunting location in the Folklore Forest region.

Hunters require a minimum Rank of Viceroy and the Mythweaver's Guide to World Domination to access Epilogue Falls.

## Gameplay Mechanics

The hunter is tasked with traversing the falls in this area. The fall is divided into 4 distinct phases: The Shore, The Rapids, Within the Waterfall, and The Hidden Grotto. To hunt in Rapids, Waterfall, and Hidden Grotto, hunter needs to build a barrel and once the run is started, it cannot be ended prematurely.

### The Shore

When the hunter enter Epilogue Falls they start in this phase. They can gather Metaphor Morsel while hunting with Standard Cheese.

### The Rapids

To get into the rapids, the hunter must build a barrel with Poetic Planks, gathered from Afterword Acres, and other materials dropped by mice in Epilogue Falls

| Type of Barrel | Constructing Requirements | Number of Hunts |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Simple Barrel | 50 Poetic Planks | 20 Hunts |
| Sturdy Barrel | 200 Poetic Planks40 Hypothetical Halophytes | 40 Hunts |
| Robust Barrel | 1000 Poetic Planks120 Hypothetical Halophytes60 Conceptual Corals | 80 Hunts |

Here, the current pushes the hunter back and the cheese gives the speed to move forward. The sum of speed by current and speed by cheese will give the net distance traveled per hunt. If the speed given by cheese is higher than the zone's current, the hunter moves forward; otherwise they will get pushed back.

| Cheese | Speed |
| --- | --- |
| Standard Cheese | 2 |
| SUPER&#124;brie+ | 4 |
| Metaphor Manchego | 10 |
| Allegory Anari | 22 |
| Symbolic Sirene | 50 |

After building the barrel, the hunter can traverse the rapids. The rapids is divided into multiple zones that have their own loot and quantity. The adjectives Sparse, Common, and Abundant denote the quantity of loot dropped in each zone. Using Condensed Creativity will only double loot dropped and has no effect on barrel's speed. The zones are not queued at random. Their order of appearing and current speed is fixed. The following table lists the order in which they appear:

| Zone Name | Start | End | Length | Current |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Sparse Morsel | 0 | 19 | 20 | -1 |
| Common Morsel | 20 | 39 | 20 | -2 |
| Abundant Morsel | 40 | 49 | 10 | -5 |
| Sparse Halophyte | 50 | 69 | 20 | -5 |
| Common Halophyte | 70 | 89 | 20 | -6 |
| Abundant Halophyte | 90 | 119 | 30 | -6 |
| Sparse Algae | 120 | 139 | 20 | -7 |
| Common Algae | 140 | 164 | 25 | -8 |
| Abundant Algae | 165 | 199 | 35 | -8 |
| Sparse Coral | 200 | 239 | 40 | -9 |
| Common Coral | 240 | 339 | 100 | -10 |
| Abundant Coral | 340 | 379 | 40 | -14 |
| Sparse Seashell | 380 | 419 | 40 | -16 |
| Common Seashell | 420 | 469 | 50 | -18 |
| Abundant Seashell | 470 | 519 | 50 | -21 |
| Sparse Plot Hook | 520 | 639 | 120 | -24 |
| Common Plot Hook | 640 | 789 | 150 | -27 |
| Abundant Plot Hook | 790 | 989 | 200 | -32 |

The hunter can also activate Jet Boost to move forward 20m per boost by spending Poetic Planks. The amount of Poetic Planks needed for each boost is listed in the table below. At the end of the run, the cost of boosting is reset.

| Boost | Cost | CumulativeCost |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 3 | 3 |
| 2 | 4 | 7 |
| 3 | 11 | 18 |
| 4 | 30 | 48 |
| 5 | 67 | 115 |
| 6 | 128 | 243 |
| 7 | 219 | 462 |
| 8 | 346 | 808 |
| 9 | 515 | 1323 |
| 10 | 732 | 2055 |
| 11 | 1003 | 3058 |
| 12 | 1334 | 4392 |
| n | 3 + (n-1)^3 |  |

### Within the Waterfall

Every cheese, usable in Epilogue Falls, can attract the mouse here but only Symbolic Sirene will push the hunter forward. The hunter cannot activate the Jet Boost here and will not get pushed back by the current even if the HUD mentions negative speed for the cheese. The current speed in this zone is 49m/hunt. Length of Waterfall is 10m extending from 990m to 999m. When a hunter enters The Waterfall, following journal entry appears

My droids have entered the Waterfall! They can no longer be pushed backwards by the current and can no longer use their Jet Boost but they'll need all the speed they can muster if they're going to push through this!

### The Hidden Grotto

The Hidden Grotto is a hidden section beyond the waterfall. The water here is still. So, the hunter can neither move forward nor backwards. The hunter have to complete rest of their hunts in this zone. When the hunter enter The Hidden Grotto, the following journal entry appears:

My droids have reached the Hidden Grotto! There is no current in here so I'm safe to hunt for as long as my barrel stays intact.

## Heads-Up Display

The HUD always shows the following information:

Top-Middle Portion

Left

- Hunter's inventory of Hypothetical Halophyte
- Hunter's inventory of Conceptual Coral
- Hunter's inventory of Plot Hook
- Hunter's inventory of Pearl of Wisdom

Clicking on the small basket icon opens up the inventory box that shows the Following Items:

- Hunter's inventory of Literary Log
- Hunter's inventory of Poetic Plank
- Hunter's inventory of Idea Germ
- Hunter's inventory of Motivation Mallet
- Hunter's inventory of Mythical Rainbow Diamond

Right

- Hunter's inventory of Condensed Creativity; which can be toggled by clicking on the "Ignite" or "Burning" button

Bottom

- Hunter's choice to automatically arm or disarm Standard Cheese after the barrel breaks. That status can be toggled by clicking on it
- Hunter's inventory of Standard Cheese; which can be changed by clicking on the arrow button on the HUD
- Hunter's inventory of Metaphor Manchego and Metaphor Morsel
- Hunter's inventory of Allegory Anari and Allegory Algae
- Hunter's inventory of Symbolic Sirene and Symbolic Seashell
- Minimum and maximum amount of loot(s) dropped in the current zone

Hunters can arm and craft the various cheeses by hovering over the corresponding HUD components and picking which recipe they prefer to craft.

Center

A small Fan icon in front of main barrel denotes 20m mark, the distance that barrel will move when Jet Boost is clicked
The rainbow colored line in front of the main barrel (inside the water) denotes net distance the barrel will move after a catch
The red line behind the main barrel (inside the water) denotes the distance barrel will go back after failing to catch
The Status Bar saying Barrel denotes the number of hunts left out of maximum hunts before that barrel would break and the current run would end

Speed of current in m/hunt

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Behold the mighty and mysterious, Epilogue Falls!

Don't let the calm waters at the shore fool you, these rapids get more and more intense the closer you get to the waterfall.

Take those Poetic Planks you earned in the Afterword Acres and use them to build barrels so you can send your droids safely(ish) up the rapids.

While riding the rapids, your droids will be able to collect many kinds of lucrative loot from the many different zones leading up to the falls.

You'll need to use stronger cheeses to move your barrel faster with each catch and you'll need stronger barrels to survive long enough to eventually reach the falls.

There are rumours that an extremely powerful guardian is lurking inside the waterfall so if you can make it all the way there then be ready for an epic battle!

I wonder what that creature is guarding...

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the Ride the Rapids and Defeat the Falls Guardian adventure in Epilogue Falls.

## Location-Specific Effects

Rainbow Quill: Provides a chance to prevent getting pushed back by the current after failing to catch. Stacks with the Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base

### Bases

- Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base
When hunter fails to catch a mouse in Rapids, the base provides a chance to prevent backward movement because of the current. This stacks with the bonus provided by Rainbow Quill from Table of Contents
- Naughty List Printing Press Base or Folklore Printing Press Base
When the Hunter has at least 1 Prolific Printing Paper in their inventory, the base's stats are boosted to:
4,500 Power
35% Power Bonus
35% Attraction Bonus
57 Luck
Consumes 1 Prolific Printing Paper per hunt

### Charms

- Baitkeep Charm - When going on a hunt with Baitkeep Charms armed, there is a chance that the charm will not be consumed and Bait will be instead

| The Marching Flame has attacked and pillaged much of the Sandtail Desert and now their warpath is headed towards Gnawnia! Their battalions attack in waves and seem to endlessly march over the horizon. All that stands between them and conquering Gnawnia are the brave hunters who camp here. |  |

## Overview

The Fiery Warpath is a location in the Sandtail Desert region requiring a minimum rank of Baron/Baroness and a Sandtail Call to Arms to enter, which can be obtained as a reward for completing the Capture the Mystic and Technic Kings Adventure in the Seasonal Garden and Zugzwang's Tower.

Mice in this location may steal points, gold, or multiple pieces of cheese if not captured.

Hunters must earn 1 Warpath Victory by defeating the Warmonger Mouse in order to travel to the Muridae Market location.

### Waves

There are four waves of mice that Hunters must defeat in order to reach the Warmonger Mouse and defeat The Marching Flame. Hunters must defeat each wave before they can face the next wave.

Waves 1 through 3
Streaks
Streaks only occur in Wave One, Wave Two, and Wave Three.
A streak is obtained by capturing the same type of mouse one after another.

A message appears in the Hunter's Journal when a streak causes mice to retreat:
I caught [X] [Mouse Name] Mice in a row! This demoralizing blow has caused [X] more of them to retreat!

Failures to catch or attract any mouse will break a streak.
Capturing any other mouse type will break a streak.
Various Fiery Warpath Charms can be used to target specific mice to help build streaks.

Failing to capture a Caravan Guard Mouse will cause one mouse of each type to be added to a wave. However, if a Hunter has one or more friends also hunting in the Fiery Warpath, reinforcements may be prevented. Catching this mouse ends a streak, but does not add mice to the wave.
Capturing a Crimson Commander Mouse will cause a number of mice equal to the current streak to retreat from each group. A streak of 0 will result in 1 mouse from each group retreating. Catching this mouse ends a streak.
When Hunters achieve a streak of 7 or more, it becomes possible to attract the Gargantuamouse. The likelihood of attracting this mouse increases as the streak grows. Catching this mouse ends a streak.

Once all mice in a wave have been defeated, Hunters will move on to the next wave of mice.

Hunters may use an Artillery Strike Launch Box to launch an Artillery Strike during Waves 1-3 and defeat an entire wave of mice.
Launching an Artillery Strike creates an entry in the Hunter's Journal:

Wave 4
Hunters must capture 12 Theurgy Warden mice before they will be able to capture the Warmonger Mouse. 
Hunters may encounter the Warmonger Mouse before capturing all 12 Theurgy Wardens, but will be unable to capture it unless they arm an Ultimate Charm.
Capturing a Warmonger Mouse will earn Hunters a Warpath Victory and will reset the Fiery Warpath to Wave One.
Warpath Victories are required for entering Muridae Market and for purchasing certain items in Muridae Market Shops and Fiery Warpath Shops.

Artillery Commander's Tent
During Wave Four Hunters may enter the Artillery Commander's Tent by using a Warpath Portal Projector.
Hunters must capture 5 Theurgy Warden mice before they will be able to capture the Artillery Commander Mouse. 
Hunters may encounter the Artillery Commander Mouse before capturing all 5 Theurgy Wardens, but will be unable to capture it unless they arm an Ultimate Charm.
Capturing an Artillery Commander Mouse will earn Hunters an Artillery Strike Launch Box and will return them to their position in Wave Four.

### Mice with Special Requirements

- Relic Hunter Mouse – This mouse visits the Fiery Warpath on an irregular basis. It does not consume cheese or charms. Hunters must have an Ancient Relic in their inventory to attract this mouse. The Ancient Relic will be taken by the Relic Hunter Mouse in exchange for a Relic Hunter Scroll Case.

- M400 – This mouse will sometimes be available in the Fiery Warpath when Hunters are actively working an M400 Hunting Research Assignment. It is attracted only to Fusion Fondue which will not attract any other mice. It drops a wide variety of loot.

## Wave One

Hunters must defeat 35 Desert Soldier mice, 35 Vanguard mice, and 35 Desert Archer mice to defeat Wave One.

The Crimson Commander and Caravan Guard will retreat once there are less than 11 mice remaining in the wave.

The Gargantuamouse can be attracted only while Hunters have a streak of 7 or more mice.

Upon completing Wave One, the following message will be displayed on the Journal:

### Wave One Mice

Mice in Wave One are primarily weak to Physical traps.
The Gargantuamouse is very unlikely to be captured using anything other than a Draconic weapon.
Fiery Warpath Charms can be used to improve the attraction of specific mice.
Standard Cheese and SUPER|brie+ will attract all mice in Wave One.
SUPER|brie+ increases attraction of the Crimson Commander mouse and reduces the attraction of the Caravan Guard mouse.

## Artillery Commander's Tent

To access the Artillery Commander's Tent, Hunters must acquire 1 Warpath Victory, 150 Flameshards, 1 Warpath Portal Core, and 1 Warpath Portal Console in order to purchase the Warpath Portal Projector. The Warpath Portal Projector will allow Hunters to enter the Artillery Commander's Tent during Wave Four. It is consumed in the process, replaced with a Ruined Portal Projector collectible.

Warpath Portal Cores and Warpath Portal Consoles are found as rewards in Warpath Treasure Chests and Rare Warpath Treasure Chests. They can be traded on the Marketplace.

Hunters must catch 5 Theurgy Wardens in the Artillery Commander's Tent before they will be able to capture the Artillery Commander.
The Artillery Commander may be encountered prior to catching all 5 Theurgy Wardens, but Hunters will not be able to capture it without using an Ultimate Charm.

Upon capturing an Artillery Commander, hunters will be returned to Wave Four and the following message appears in the journal:

To reenter the Artillery Commander's Tent Hunters will need to purchase another Warpath Portal Projector. Hunters may enter the Artillery Commander's Tent multiple times without affecting their position in Wave Four.

### Artillery Commander's Tent Mice

Theurgy Warden Mouse is primarily weak to Physical traps while Artillery Commander Mouse is primarily weak to Arcane traps.
Standard Cheese and SUPER|brie+ will attract both mice in Artillery Commander's Tent
The Artillery Commander Mouse is guaranteed to drop an Artillery Strike Launch Box each time it is caught.

| Artillery Commander | 250,000 | 500,000 | 1 | Artillery Strike Launch Box |

## Hunting Strategy

Hunters must defeat waves of The Marching Flame in order to confront and defeat the Warmonger Mouse.
Mice in the Fiery Warpath are attracted to Standard Cheese and SUPER|brie+, though the higher attraction rate of SUPER|brie+ is preferable when trying to build streaks.
A variety of Fiery Warpath Charms are available to Hunters to help defeat the mice of The Marching Flame.

### Waves

Each wave must be defeated before Hunters can continue on to the next wave.
Hunters are rewarded with a Warpath Commander's Charm for defeating each wave except the 4th.
Defeating the 4th wave by capturing the Warmonger Mouse rewards Hunters with 1 Warpath Victory.

- Wave 1

- Wave 2

- Wave 3

- Wave 4

- Artillery Commander's Tent

### Streaks

Building streaks is the quickest way to defeat the mice found in the Fiery Warpath.
A streak is obtained by capturing the same class of mouse one after another.

Failures to catch or attract any mouse will break a streak.
Capturing any other mouse type will break a streak.
Various Fiery Warpath Charms can be used to target specific mice to help build streaks.

Mice that are forced to the retreat do not count as "caught" for a Hunter's statistics. They simply leave the area, reducing the number of mice Hunters need to catch to defeat the wave.
Leaving the Fiery Warpath will not affect a streak.

### Artillery Strike

During Waves 1 - 3 Hunters may launch an Artillery Strike if they have an Artillery Strike Launch Box in their inventory. An Artillery Strike cannot be launched to defeat Wave 4 mice.

Launching an Artillery Strike will consume the Artillery Strike Launch Box and defeat the current Wave of mice instantly, allowing the Hunter to move to the next Wave of mice.

### Support Mice

Support mice cannot form streaks, but can add and subtract mice from the current Wave. These mice are only found in waves 1 - 3.
Support mice retreat when the size of the Wave is reduced to a certain point. They can no longer be attracted after this point is reached.

There are two Support mice.

- Caravan Guard Mouse

- Crimson Commander Mouse

### Gargantuamouse

When Hunters achieve a streak of 7 or more in waves 1 - 3, it becomes possible to attract the Gargantuamouse. The likelihood of attracting this mouse increases as the streak grows. Catching this mouse ends a streak.

This mouse's primary weakness is to Draconic traps. Arcane, Hydro, Physical, and Tactical traps are less effective and are much less likely to capture this mouse.

Using a Gargantua Charm will reduce the population to just the Gargantuamouse, but will not guarantee that Hunters catch the mouse. It is still possible to fail to attract a mouse with this charm, so Hunters should arm SUPER|brie+ for the best possible attraction rate. This charm is always consumed, so Hunters should be prepared to disarm their supply immediately after taking a hunt.

When a hunter fails to capture a Gargantuamouse, the following message will appear on the Hunter's Journal:

The Gargantuamouse cannot be encountered in Wave 4.

### Location-Specific Effects

#### Weapons

- The Warpath Thrasher is a Limited Edition trap that provides a bonus of 4500 power and 24 luck in waves 1-3 of the Fiery Warpath. The extra power and luck effect is quickly reduced to zero as the number of available mice is reduced to 50% of the starting total of all mice in the wave.
- The Warden Slayer Trap is a Limited Edition trap that provides +2,500 power and +2,500% power bonus during Wave Four.

#### Charms

- Archer Charms

- Cavalry Charms

- Commander Charms

- Gargantuamouse Charms

- Mage Charms

- Scout Charms

- Warmonger Charms

- Warrior Charms

- Miscellaneous Charms

### Adventure Book

Hunters must complete Capture the Mystic and Technic Kings in the Seasonal Garden and Zugzwang's Tower to obtain the Sandtail Call to Arms in order to enter the Fiery Warpath.
Hunters can Push Back The Marching Flame by catching the Warmonger Mouse and obtaining a Warpath Victory.

### Library Assignments

- M400 Hunting Research Assignment - This assignment may require collecting 1 Piece of M400 Intel from mice in the Fiery Warpath and/or capturing the M400.

### Tournaments

Fiery Warpath mice are worth points during some Tournaments.

### Treasure Maps

Many mice in the Fiery Warpath are often found on Muridae Protector Treasure Maps, Relic Hunter Treasure Maps and Warpath Treasure Maps.

### Hunting tips by Larry

The Marching Flame are preparing to invade Gnawnia. Their Fiery Warpath to the castle has reached a standoff in the Sandtail Desert where you must hold them back at all costs! These mice are highly organized, you must defeat each wave before progressing to the next.

Capturing the same type of mouse multiple times in a row will demoralize the troops, causing that type to regroup off the battlefield. The higher your streak, the more will retreat. Capturing a Crimson Commander while having a high streak will cause even more to retreat as they carry the commands throughout the battalion.

The King has stocked a Charm Shoppe that will help you achieve these high streaks and outmanoeuvre your enemy. The Marching Flame is equally prepared, though. Failing to capture Caravan Guard Mice will allow reinforcements to arrive. According to the King's scouts, SUPER|brie+ is known to attract more Crimson Commanders and fewer Caravan Guards.

Within the fourth and final wave is their leader, a Warmonger Mouse. Be warned that although the Warmonger Mouse can be encountered at any time during this wave, they will be immune to capture until you have defeated all of the Theurgy Warden Mice that guard them. When you succeed in catching a Warmonger Mouse, the rewards will be great. However, after capturing your first, the Warmongers will be keen to your threat and will guard their loot more closely.

Best of luck hunter! No victory is too small and no defeat is too great. One mouse or one hunter could turn the tide of the war. Gnawnia needs you to succeed!

# 📍 Location: Floating Islands

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Floating Islands](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Floating_Islands)

| Floating Islands |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Hollow Heights |  |
| Minimum Rank: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Travel Requirement: | Dirigible and Flight License |
| Recommended Power Types: | ArcaneForgottenHydroShadowDraconicLawPhysicalTactical |
| Recommended Cheese: | Cloud CheesecakeExtra Rich Cloud Cheesecake |
| Number of Mice: | 71 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Lose Gold: | Yes |
| Lose Points: | Yes |
| Lose Cheese: | Yes |
| Release Date: | 8 September 2020 |
| An archipelago of airborne islands that shift positions with the prevailing winds. This bountiful skyward region was previously cut off from the land-dwellers of Gnawnia due to the impenetrable magical storms created by Ful’Mina. Any intrepid hunter who possesses the Skyfarer's Oculus, a dirigible, and the protective Warden Stones will now be able to sail safely through the storms to this lofty paradise in the clouds. |  |

## Overview

Floating Islands is a Location located within the Hollow Heights Region, requiring a minimum Rank of Archduke/Archduchess and the Dirigible and Flight License Map Piece to access.

Floating Islands makes use of 8 different Power Types, nearly every available one in the game, namely:

- Arcane
- Forgotten
- Hydro
- Shadow
- Draconic
- Law
- Physical
- Tactical

Mice here are able to pillage Standard Cheese, Points and Gold if one fails to capture them.

Hunters first collect Cloud Curd to purchase Cloud Cheesecake while at the Launch Pad. All 8 Power Types are effective here.
Subsequently, Hunters fly towards specific Power Type Islands to hunt mice using their Sky Map.
There are 2 versions of each Island, Low Altitude and High Altitude: Hunters must first clear a minimum of 4 Low Altitude Islands and defeat 4 different Wardens to unlock a single High Altitude Island.
While hunting on the islands, mice will drop loot used to upgrade your Skyfarer's Oculus, customise your Dirigible and obtain new Traps.

When Hunters have completed the Explore the Floating Islands Adventure, they can proceed to purchase the High Altitude Flight License from the Cartographer.
Together with 500 Sky Glass and 500 Sky Ore, Hunters can then attach Rocket Boosters onto their Dirigible.
These boosters enable Paragons, Pirates and Richard the Rich in both Low Altitude and High Altitude islands to drop Storm Cells.
Once the Hunter has collected 50 Storm Cells, they can be spent on the Launch Console while at the Launch Pad to journey to the Empyrean Sky Palace instead of a regular island.
The Empyrean Sky Palace consists of different Power Type Vaults which have mice that drop greater amounts of loot as compared to those obtained on regular islands.

## HUD

The HUD always shows the Following Items:

- Hunter's inventory of Cloud Curd
- Hunter's inventory of Cloud Cheesecake
- Hunter's inventory of Extra Rich Cloud Cheesecake
- Hunter's inventory of Sky Ore
- Hunter's inventory of Sky Glass
- Hunter's inventory of Corsair's Curd
- Hunter's inventory of Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese
- Number of Sky Wardens remaining

Hunters can configure their Skyfarer's Satchel to show following items:

- Hunter's inventory of Bottled Wind
- Hunter's inventory of Cyclone Stone
- Hunter's inventory of Sky Sprocket
- Hunter's inventory of Skysoft Silk
- Hunter's inventory of Enchanted Wing
- Hunter's inventory of Cloudstone Bangle
- Hunter's inventory of Sky Pirate Seal
- Hunter's inventory of Raw Ancient Jade (available once hunter completes Explore the Floating Islands adventure)
- Hunter's inventory of Empyrean Seal (available once hunter completes Explore the Floating Islands adventure)
- Hunter's inventory of Adorned Empyrean Jewel (available once hunter completes Explore the Floating Islands adventure)
- Hunter's inventory of Storm Cell (available once hunter completes Explore the Floating Islands adventure)
- Hunter's inventory of Low Altitude Treasure Trove
- Hunter's inventory of High Altitude Treasure Trove
- Hunter's inventory of Empyrean Treasure Trove (available once hunter claims Explore the Floating Islands adventure)

While on the Launch Pad:

- Hunters can access the Workshop on the HUD to upgrade their Oculus or to change the cosmetic skins on their Dirigible. Their Oculus level is displayed above the Workshop icon
- The Sky Map is located here. It allows Hunters to select which Power Type Island they want to visit.
- There is also a Log that tracks the progress of a previous run.

While on Islands

- The HUD tracks the progress while on Low Altitude Islands, High Altitude Islands, or Sky Palace Vaults. It is represented by a progress bar made up of smaller boxes.
- Clicking on the Dirigible opens the Workshop

### Trap Library

Hunters will be able to save specific setups for every Island and the Launchpad. This feature will automatically change a Hunter's trap setup to the saved one for the corresponding Island or Launch Pad upon hitting the respective sub-location. Components that can be saved include:

- Cheese
- Weapon
- Base
- Charm
- Skin

If a Hunter does not have any more of the saved Cheese or Charm remaining in their inventory, it will be highlighted with a red box. All other components will still be changed.
If a hunter refunds a saved Weapon or Base, that component will also be highlighted in red while the other available ones will not be changed.

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Launch Pad

On the Launch Pad, Hunters will be able to hunt mice to collect Cloud Curd to purchase Cloud Cheesecake with.

Here, the Workshop, Skymap and Log can be accessed.

All Launch Pad mice are weak against all Power Types except Parental and Rift.

#### Workshop

While exploring islands you'll find Sky Ore and Sky Glass which you can use to upgrade your Skyfarer's Oculus. Upgrades will add a greater quantity and variety of Island features to the Sky Map. As you upgrade your Skyfarer's Oculus the Islands you're able to visit become more diverse and lucrative. This is reflected by more modifiers appearing on your Sky Map

#### Airship Cosmetics

You can also customise your Dirigible here with various cosmetic skins found while hunting. There are 3 customisable parts: Balloon, Sails and Hull.

See here for Airship Cosmetics table.

#### Sky Map

Opening the Sky Map on the Launch Pad allows a hunter to choose between 8 unique, randomised islands to visit. There is one island for each of the 8 Power Types and the unique checkerboard interface shows what kind of loot you'll find on each of the islands. On the same board are all the modifiers available to the Hunter via upgrades at the Workshop.

Your main objective when selecting and visiting an island is to defeat the four Sky Wardens: Fog, Wind, Rain, and Frost. Once one is defeated it's removed from the Sky Map and once all four are defeated your Dirigible will be able to ascend to the more lucrative High Altitude Islands.

The Sky Map is split into 2 orientations:

- Horizontally from the left are the Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Shadow Islands.
- Vertically from the bottom are the Draconic, Law, Physical, Tactical.

Hunters are encouraged to select which Islands they would like to visit based on the loot and modifiers available on said Island. When selecting an Island, all loot and modifiers highlighted by the interface on the specific horizontal row or vertical column associated with the Power Type can be found on the Island. Be sure to select an Island with a Warden Shrine available. They are depicted by the Hexagonal runes. Every Warden Shrine will always be available on 2 different Islands, one horizontal, one vertical. Hunters are advised to pick the one where they have got a better trap set up for. However, if simply after loot, Hunters may opt to pick an island with the most loot modifiers rather than a Warden Shrine

Pay attention to the order of the loot and modifier as it appears in the same order while hunting on an Island. Horizontal from left to right and Vertical from bottom to top. IE the modifier nearer to the Power Type icon on the Sky Map will always appear first on the Island.

Hunters can use Cyclone Stones to reroll their Sky Map to obtain a more favourable loot and modifier layout that suits their needs.

### While in Flight

- Regardless of whether the Hunter has opted for a Low Altitude Island, High Altitude Island or a Sky Palace Vault, the Hunter will have 75 hunts maximum in flight.
The Hunter can choose to spend all 75 hunts or retreat, though the latter option is only available after the Hunter has progressed far enough in the island or vault (see below).
- Based on the Hunter's selection on the Sky Map or the Launch Console, the modifiers picked are now enlarged on the HUD in the same order.
The HUD serves as a progress bar, showing how far the Hunter has explored the island or vault while in flight.
Each modifier is now represented by a large box that consists of 10 smaller bars.
Modifiers are activated upon different stages of progress:
The first modifier will be automatically activated upon the start of the flight
The second modifier becomes active after the Hunter attains 10 green/blue bars
The third modifier becomes active after the Hunter attains 20 green/blue bars
The last modifier becomes active after the Hunter attains 30 green/blue bars
It is possible for Low Altitude Islands to contain boxes without any modifiers in them if the Hunter has selected as such in the Sky Map.
Hunters having an Oculus Level of 6 and below can also potentially have High Altitude Islands with no-modifier boxes.
- With each hunt, Hunters explore the island or vault by moving along the progress bar:
Each successful hunt moves the Hunter by 1 smaller bar from left to right (represented in green, or blue if Bottled Wind is enabled):
If Bottled Wind is enabled: +1 smaller bar
(for Low Altitude Islands) If Shrine of Fog/Frost/Rain/Wind is active: +1 smaller bar
(for High Altitude Islands) If Paragon Bangle/Silk/Sprocket/Wing Shrine is active: +1 smaller bar
With Bottled Wind enabled and Shrine active: +2 smaller bars
Each failure to catch moves the Hunter by 1 smaller bar from right to left (represented in red)
Each failure to attract does not affect the Hunter's progress, though a hunt out of the maximum 75 will still be spent
- Enabling Bottled Wind will cause some mice loot to be boosted by 1: see this for details.
- When the green/blue and red (if any) bars meet on the HUD:
Only one mouse can be encountered by the Hunter until it is caught (this mouse can only be caught once per flight and only at this stage):
Sky Warden for Low Altitude Islands (depending on Shrine picked)
If the Hunter did not pick any Shrine, then no Warden will be encountered at this stage and the Hunter will attract mice as per usual
Sky Paragon for High Altitude Islands (depending on power type picked)
Empyrean Empress for Sky Palace vaults
Upon reaching this stage in the island or vault, the Hunter can choose to retreat if they wish to.
Please be warned that it is possible for a Hunter to retreat without catching the relevant mouse: Hunters are advised to be careful as retreating is a one-way process.
From this stage onwards, each hunt works slightly differently as compared to before until all the bars are green/blue:
Each successful hunt will still move the Hunter similarly but green/blue bars will replace red bars
Each failure to catch will still move the Hunter similarly but red bars will replace green/blue bars until the start of the box where the Hunter encountered the relevant mouse
Each failure to attract works similarly as before
It is possible for a Hunter to have no green/blue or red bars if the Hunter does not catch or miss any mice respectively: the relevant mouse will then only be encountered when all 40 small bars are green/blue or red.
- When all the bars are green/blue, each hunt will no longer have any effect on the progress bar. The Hunter will also be rewarded with the following for fully exploring the island or vault:
Low Altitude Treasure Trove for Low Altitude Islands
High Altitude Treasure Trove for High Altitude Islands
Empyrean Treasure Trove for Sky Palace vaults

### Hunting tips by Larry

Welcome to the Floating Islands where you can finally see the world from Ronza's perspective!

Now that you've got your flight license, the sky is the limit! The King has commissioned the construction of this luxurious Launch Pad (it took a lot of tariffs to pull this one off!) as a home for hunters who are taking to the skies to capture the mice above the clouds!

Here you will find a new type of building: the Workshop. It can be used to upgrade your Skyfarer's Oculus and customize your dirigible. You can also find the usual assortment of shops which will be crucial in providing much needed bait, trap upgrades, and some special supplies for sailing the skies!

The Cheese Shoppe has been stocked with a delicious new cheesy treat that is so light and fluffy that it defies the laws of physics! I suggest loading up on at least 75 pieces for your first trip to the islands.

The Charm Shoppe has some pretty powerful charms for sale so it's worth a visit for the veteran Floating Islander.

The Trapsmith should be one of your first stops so you can see what new wonders of the mouse catching world are available for purchase as well as what resources they require from the various islands above.

Now, before you go rushing off to hit that shiny new "Start" button, let me share the intel we've gathered so far about how the islands seem to work.

Upon opening your Sky Map, you will see the current layout of islands available to you as well as Markers indicating unique spots that are located on each one. Each island is also home to a specific group of mice that are weak to the power type indicated on the axis of the Sky Map grid. Remember, the winds of change are constantly blowing, after each island you explore, the Sky Map will change and a new set of islands will appear so don't take this decision lightly!

An island with a shrine on it will also be home to one of the four Wardens. These Wardens prevent travel to the High Altitude islands which are home to the most powerful mice and rarest resources. You will need to capture all four Wardens before your Sky Map will reveal the High Altitude islands. Make sure to gather as many resources as possible along the way so that you will be well prepared to battle the powerful guardians of the High Altitude islands: the Paragons!

After selecting an island and pressing "Launch", you will set sail in your dirigible and land at your destination. Upon landing, make sure to adjust your trap setup to the power type of that island. To make this easier, your dirigible has been equipped with a brand new Trap Library that you can use to save your complete setup for each power type. Once you've saved it, that setup will equip automatically every time you visit an island with that power type! This fancy new feature should help to make your island voyages much smoother! Once you've landed and equipped your setup, you can begin exploring these miraculous places! Be careful though, you only have 75 hunts before the island floats too far away from the Launch Pad thus preventing a safe return. If you exceed this limit, your crew will automatically pack everything up and take you back to the Launch Pad. So, make every hunt count!

Each successful catch on the island will progress your exploration bar but each time you fail to catch, the enemy mice on the other side of the island will make progress towards you!
If you are successful enough to enter a special area of the island, you will immediately gain its bonus for the remainder of your hunts. However, the mice are exploring it as well but they are looking for you! If you meet up with that enemy hoard and there is a shrine on the island, it will be the Warden that leads the charge! Once you meet a Warden, you won't be able to attract any other mice until you either catch it or are forced off the island!

Fully exploring an area of the island will fortify its location which means the opposing mice cannot push you back any further.

Well, I guess that's it -- oh wait, I almost forgot! There have been reports of Sky Pirates roaming the skies in this region so be extra vigilant in your expeditions!

The rest is up to you. Good luck, hunter!

Wow... what a view! To think, I almost missed out on this due to my crippling fear of heights!

### Location-Specific Effects

#### Auras

- Jet Stream Aura
Grants +1 Speed on Low and High Altitude Islands
Makes Sky Paragons drop additional 8 Storm Cells
Makes Sky Wardens drop 4 Storm Cells

#### Traps

- Polar Vortex Trap
Adds +1 to area-specific loot drops
Consumes 1 Cyclone Stone per catch
- C.L.A.W. Machine Trap
Doubles loot drops of Corsair's Curd and Sky Pirate Seal.

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete the following adventures in Floating Islands:

- Explore the Floating Islands
- Collapse the Empyrean Empire

## Mice

- All 8 Power Types are effective on Launch Pad, Cloud Commoners, Sky Wardens, Sky Pirates and The Richest.
- Sky Wardens and Sky Pirates can be found on any Island as long as the respective modifier is available.
Mice only drop Cloud Curd with the Level 2 and 6 Oculus upgrade while visiting an island with the respective modifier avaliable.

# 📍 Location: Folklore Forest

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Folklore Forest](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Folklore_Forest)

Folklore Forest is a region released on 26 July 2022.
It consists of an area in the north-west region of the map, surrounded by Furoma and Bristle Woods to the north, Gnawnia and Whisker Woods to the east, and Sandtail Desert to the south.

There are eight locations in this region:

- Foreword Farm

- Prologue Pond

- Table of Contents

- Bountiful Beanstalk

- School of Sorcery

- Draconic Depths

- Afterword Acres

- Epilogue Falls

- Conclusion Cliffs

# 📍 Location: Forbidden Grove

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Forbidden Grove](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Forbidden_Grove)

The Forbidden Grove is on a 20 hour cycle. It remains open for 16 hours, then closes for 4 hours. Hunters may not travel to the Forbidden Grove while it is closed.

| Forbidden Grove |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Bristle Woods |  |

| Lose Points: | Yes |

| Release Date: | 4 March 2009 |
| At the end of the Catacombs, beyond heavy stone gates, lies a hidden hunting ground. Etched into the gates is a warning: "Beware young soul, should the stone gates close while within this grove, you will find yourself caught between realms. |  |

## Overview

The Forbidden Grove is a location in the Bristle Woods region requiring a minimum rank of Legendary and the Keeper's Candle to enter. In addition, the Forbidden Grove must be open for Hunters to access the location.
The Keeper's Candle can be acquired by capturing a Keeper Mouse in the Catacombs.
Mice in the Forbidden Grove may steal points, gold, or multiple pieces of cheese if not captured.

The Forbidden Grove acts as a gateway to the Acolyte Realm. Hunters may not entered the Acolyte Realm without first catching a Realm Ripper Mouse in the Forbidden Grove.

### Open/Close Cycle

The Forbidden Grove is on a 20 hour cycle. It remains open for 16 hours, then closes for 4 hours. Hunters may not travel to the Forbidden Grove while it is closed.
When the Forbidden Grove is open, the Realm Ripper Mouse cannot be encountered unless the Hunter has a Realm Ripper Charm armed. This prevents Hunters from entering the Acolyte Realm.
When the Forbidden Grove closes, the Realm Ripper Mouse can be encountered and is the only mouse available. Capturing the Realm Ripper Mouse transports the Hunter to the Acolyte Realm and is the only way Hunters are able to enter the Acolyte Realm location.

### Heads-Up Display

The HUD in the Forbidden Grove tracks the time remaining in the open/close cycle before the location closes. It also tracks the number of Runes and Runic Cheese in the Hunter's inventory.

## Forbidden Grove Mice

The Forbidden Grove mice are a mix of mice from The Forgotten Mice, The Shadow Clan, and the Indigenous Mice groups.
Mice found in this location are primarily weak to Arcane and Shadow weapons, with a secondary weakness to Forgotten weapons. The Hunter's choice of cheese will determine which weapon to arm for best results.

### Realm Ripper Mouse

The Realm Ripper Mouse is only available once the Forbidden Grove has closed unless the Hunter has a Realm Ripper Charm armed. It is also the only mouse that can be encountered once the Forbidden Grove closes.
Capturing a Realm Ripper Mouse transports the Hunter to the Acolyte Realm and is the only way Hunters can travel to that location.

### Cheese Preference and Loot

Radioactive Blue Cheese, Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese and Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese attracts the relatively weaker mice found in the Forbidden Grove. These mice are weakest to Shadow weapons.
Ancient Cheese attracts almost all mice in the Forbidden Grove. Arcane weapons are strongly recommended when hunting with Ancient Cheese.
Moon Cheese significantly reduces the mouse population in the Forbidden Grove.

## Hunting Strategy

The Forbidden Grove is on a 20 hour cycle. It remains open for 16 hours, then closes for 4 hours. Hunters may not travel to the Forbidden Grove while it is closed.

Hunters will need to capture a Realm Ripper Mouse in the Forbidden Grove if they wish to enter the Acolyte Realm. The Realm Ripper Mouse is only available once the Forbidden Grove has closed. unless a Realm Ripper Charm is armed. If a Hunter's trap check occurs at the same time as the closing of the Forbidden Grove, the Realm Ripper Mouse can be attracted and caught on the trap check itself.

Mice found in this location are primarily weak to Arcane and Shadow weapons, with a secondary weakness to Forgotten weapons. The Hunter's choice of cheese will determine which weapon to arm for best results.

- Radioactive Blue Cheese attracts the relatively weaker mice found in the Forbidden Grove. These mice are weakest to Shadow weapons
- Ancient Cheese attracts almost all mice in the Forbidden Grove. Arcane weapons are strongly recommended when hunting with Ancient Cheese
- Moon Cheese significantly reduces the mouse population in the Forbidden Grove.

### Location-Specific Effects

#### Bases

- The Runic Base is a limited edition base that increases the chance of Runes being dropped as loot, as well as adding a small chance of receiving an extra Rune.

### Adventure Book

Hunters can complete steps to Capture the Acolyte Mouse by obtaining Runes to craft Runic Cheese and by catching a Realm Ripper in the Forbidden Grove.

### Library Assignments

- M400 Bait Research Assignment - This assignment may require Hunters to track down specific items from Forbidden Grove mice in order to complete it.
- M400 Hunting Research Assignment - This assignment may require collecting 1 Piece of M400 Intel from mice in the Forbidden Grove and/or capture the M400.
- Zurreal Trap Research Assignment - This assignment requires Hunters to collect 3 Warp Nails from the Realm Ripper Mouse in the Forbidden Grove. This will require 3 visits to the Forbidden Grove.

### Tournaments

Forbidden Grove mice are worth points during some Tournaments.

### Treasure Maps

Many mice in the Forbidden Grove are often found on Acolyte Treasure Maps and Relic Hunter Treasure Maps.

### Hunting tips by Larry

Only the most capable MouseHunters make it to the Forbidden Grove! I'm impressed.

This area may look like a garden, but it is actually a giant trap designed to catch the powerful Acolyte mouse! Every few hours the massive stone gates close, sealing this area off from the Catacombs. When the gates close, a thick fog fills the grove and anyone still inside will be transported into the Acolyte Realm upon capturing a Realm Ripper Mouse.

The mice here are weak to Arcane traps and are highly attracted to Ancient Cheese. To make Ancient Cheese, combine three Stale cheese with six Ionized Salt. You can also add three Magic Essence to the same recipe to double the amount of cheese produced.

The mice within this grove are trying to reverse the effects of the portal to keep a two-way gate open at all times. Should they accomplish this goal, armies of mice will soon invade Gnawnia at the command of the evil Acolyte!

Be on the look out for magical runes dropped by the mice here. Runes are used to craft Runic cheese, which is the only cheese capable of attracting an Acolyte.

Remember that when the gates close, Realm Ripper mice will be in the grove. If you capture a Realm Ripper, you will be transported to the Acolyte Realm. Keep this fact in mind before leaving your trap for long periods of time.

# 📍 Location: Foreword Farm

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Foreword Farm](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Foreword_Farm)

| Foreword Farm |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Folklore Forest |  |
| Rank requirement: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Map piece: | Legendary Looking Glass |
| Required Power Type: | Tactical |
| Required Cheese: | Standard Cheese |
| Number of Mice: | 9 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Release Date: | 26 July 2022 |
| Tend these fertile fields to grow a bountiful harvest of Parable Papyrus! |  |

## Overview

Foreword Farm is a hunting location in Folklore Forest region.

Foreword Farm, Prologue Pond, and Table of Contents are 3 connected hunting locations.
They require the minimum rank of Archduke/Archduchess and the Legendary Looking Glass to access.

Parable Papyrus earned here can be used to purchase different type of cheese to use in Table of Contents.
Crop Coin earned here can be used to unlock several Upgrades in those 3 locations.

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Planting

In Foreword Farm, the Planting Mechanics are in effect.

- There are 3 planting plots.
The first plot is available as soon as the Hunter enters this location.
The second and third plot can be unlocked by spending certain resources in Upgrades.
- There are 6 types of plants.
- Planting consumes a cost and places a plant into one plot.
Each plot can hold 1 active plant and 1 queued plant.
Plants grow by catching mice.
Newly placed plants start at 0 cm.
Each catch grows active plants by 10 cm. This can be doubled to 20 cm with the use of Condensed Creativity.
Once a plant reaches 200 cm, it will mature, and:
The yield is added to the harvest bin.
The queued plant becomes active.
- The Hunter can choose to uproot a plant. Doing so will remove the plant from the plot and refund its cost.
- The number and type of active plants determine available mice.

#### Info interface

Each tab provides information on what to do in each location.

Tend your fields and harvest Parable Papyrus in the Foreword Farm!

Hunt with any standard bait to collect Papyrus Seeds.

Use Papyrus Seeds to plant Mythical Mulch Plants.

Defend your crops from the hungry pest mice. Each catch will grow your plants!

Harvest Mythical Mulch and use it with Papyrus Seeds to plant Parable Papyrus Plants.

Grow your plants to maturity and harvest Parable Papyrus.

Go fishing for Inspiration Ink at the Prologue Pond!

Hunt with any standard bait to collect Ingenuity Grubs then use them to craft Grubbeen Cheese.

Hunt with Grubbeen Cheese to collect Pond Pennies and use them to craft Brainstorm Chum.

Scatter Brainstorm Chum while hunting with Grubbeen cheese to collect Cleverness Clams.

Use Cleverness Clams to craft Clamembert Cheese and use it with Brainstorm Chum to collect Inspiration Ink.

Write your masterpiece in the Table of Contents to earn Gnawbel Prizes for Literature!

Start writing a book with any standard bait and collect Draft Derby Curds.

Catch mice to write words! The mouse's point value will determine how many words you write per catch.

Use Draft Derby Curds with Parable Papyrus and Inspiration Ink to craft the various tiers of Draft Derby Cheese.

Each tier of Draft Derby cheese will attract mice with higher point values, allowing you to write longer books.

Longer books will reward more Draft Derby Curds and more Gnawbel Prizes for Literature!

Once you reach the Encyclopedia of Lore, you can use Final Draft Derby to attract the Mythweaver!

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Greetings hunter and welcome to the Foreword Farm!

The soil here seems to be enchanted and is unlike anywhere else in the Kingdom! Under the right conditions, you should be able to grow and harvest a new and very valuable crop called Parable Papyrus!

Start by collecting Papyrus Seeds from the Seed Stower mice that are wandering the fields and use them to plant Mythical Mulch Plants. Once you start growing any kind of plant, you'll begin to attract the Petulant Pest mice who have an insatiable appetite for your delicious crops. The more plants you grow, the more pests you'll attract so be ready for these ravenous rascals. Defend your plants from these pests and grow them to maturity in order to collect their bountiful harvest.

Harvesting Mythical Mulch Plants will earn you a potent material called... you guessed it, Mythical Mulch! By planting Papyrus Seeds and Mythical Mulch together, you can grow the extremely valuable Parable Papyrus Plant! The Parable Papyrus that you harvest from this precious plant will become very useful when you get to the Table of Contents.

There is another type of plant available to cultivate here but hunters should be warned that although it's quite valuable to harvest, planting too many of these Twisted plants may attract a very dangerous adversary to your field!

Happy hunting and harvesting!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete steps of the Outwrite the Mythweaver adventure in Foreword Farm.

# 📍 Location: Fort Rox

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Fort Rox](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Fort_Rox)

| Fort Rox |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Varmint Valley |  |
| Minimum Rank: | Baron/Baroness |
| Travel Requirement: | Fort Rox Battle Summons |
| Recommended Power Types: | ArcaneShadowLaw |
| Recommended Cheeses: | Crescent CheeseMoon CheeseSunrise Cheese |
| Number of Mice: | 25 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Lose Gold: | Yes |
| Lose Points: | Yes |
| Lose Cheese: | Yes |
| Release Date: | 3 November 2016 |
| A strange meteor has landed near Claw Show City, leaving a huge impact crater near the Gnawnian Express rails! This mysterious meteor brings with it a mysterious new breed of mice and their presence threatens the lives of the citizens of Varmint Valley. Around the crater, an encampment of hunters has surrounded these dangerous mice and are setting up a fort to hold them back! |  |

## Overview

Fort Rox is a hunting location in Varmint Valley region.

It requires the minimum Rank of Baron/Baroness and the Fort Rox Battle Summons to access.

There are 5 defensive features in this area:

- The Wall determines the Hunter's survivability during Night and Dawn.
- The Ballista improves the catch rate against Weremice and Nightmancer.
- The Cannon improves the catch rate against Cosmic Critter and Nightfire.
- The Moat reduces damage to the Wall.
- The Mage Tower provides various effects to hunting and progressing in this area.

There are 4 phases in this area:

- During Day, the Hunter can collect Meteorite Piece, repair Wall and upgrade Fort.
- During Night, the Hunter can collect Howlite and Bloodstone to upgrade Fort when return to Day.
- During Dawn, the Hunter can collect Dawn Dust to purchase Map Piece for the next area as well as to access a harder challenge in Fort Rox.
- During Heart of the Meteor, the Hunter can hunt for Meteoric Core Fragments, which is required to purchase a new Law weapon in the Trapsmith.

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Fort

The Fort represents the Hunter's various defensive features.

Each of the defensive features can be upgraded to provide greater benefits and effects. Upgrades for the individual defensive features must be made in order. Upgrades may only be made during the Day.

The effects of each upgrade combine with those of previous upgrades, except for the Wall and Moat.

#### Wall

Bigger and better walls have higher maximum health. If your wall falls, you'll have to retreat and repair it before returning to defend your fort.

| Upgrade | Description | Cost |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gold | MeteoritePiece | Howlite | Bloodstone |  |  |
| Basic Wall | This provides you with a Basic Wall that has 50 health. | 30,000 | 50 |  |  |
| Howlite Wall | This increases your maximum possible wall health to 100! | 45,000 |  | 8 |  |
| Blood Bastion | This increases your maximum possible wall health to 200! | 98,000 |  | 35 | 15 |
| Moonbeam Fortress | This increases your maximum possible wall health to 350! | 128,000 |  | 120 | 60 |

#### Ballista

The Ballista will boost your effectiveness against Weremice, most commonly found during Twilight and Midnight.

| Upgrade | Description | Cost |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gold | Howlite | Bloodstone |  |  |
| Basic Ballista | Decreases the power of Fort Rox Weremice by 50%. | 30,000 | 1 |  |
| Silver Ballista | Provides a chance to instantly capture Fort Rox Weremice. | 45,000 | 20 |  |
| Sunsilver Ballista | Instantly defeats the Nightmancer Mouse. | 98,000 | 70 | 20 |

#### Cannon

The Cannon will boost your effectiveness against Cosmic Critters, most commonly found during Utter Darkness and First Light.

| Upgrade | Description | Cost |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gold | Howlite | Bloodstone |  |  |
| Small Cannon | Decreases the power of Fort Rox Cosmic Critters by 50%. | 30,000 | 15 | 5 |
| Power Cannon | Provides a chance to instantly capture Fort Rox Cosmic Critters. | 45,000 | 25 | 12 |
| Energy Cannon | Instantly defeats the Nightfire Mouse. | 98,000 | 40 | 50 |

#### Moat

The Moat reduces the amount of damage your wall takes during the night.

| Upgrade | Description | Cost |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gold | Howlite | Bloodstone |  |  |
| Small Moat | Reduces damage your wall receives by 1. | 30,000 | 5 |  |
| Big Moat | Reduces damage your wall receives by 2. | 45,000 | 30 | 2 |
| Silver Moat | Reduces damage your wall receives by 3. | 98,000 | 70 | 35 |

#### Mage Tower

The Mage Tower can be enchanted with special abilities. While active, it'll use 1 Tower Mana per hunt and apply all unlocked abilities.

| Upgrade | Description | Cost |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gold | Howlite | Bloodstone | Dawn Dust |  |  |
| Spotlight Enchantment | Doubles loot drops when Meteorite Piece, Howlite, or Bloodstone is found while the Mage Tower is active. | 5,000 |  |  |  |
| Power Enchantment | Adds a chance to instantly capture any mouse while the Mage Tower is active. | 40,000 | 1 |  |  |
| Healing Enchantment | Heals your wall by 1 health after every hunt while the Mage Tower is active. | 50,000 | 10 | 1 |  |
| Sunsphere Enchantment | Reduces hunts until dawn by 2 after every hunt while the Mage Tower is active. | 90,000 |  | 10 | 1 |

### Phases

#### Day

This is the starting phase in Fort Rox. The Hunter can return to this phase from Night and Dawn when certain conditions are met.

The Fort can be upgraded only during Day.

The Wall can be repaired only during Day. Each Meteorite Piece repairs one damage to the Wall. Unless the quantity of Meteorite Pieces is insufficient to fully repair a Wall, it is not possible to partially repair a wall.

Hunting during Day does not affect the Wall health.

The Mage Tower can be activated, but the Healing Enchantment and Sunsphere Enchantment have no effect during Day.

The Hunter can choose to enter Night from this phase.

#### Night

The Hunter can enter this phase by choosing to Defend Fort from Day phase.

The Night can be divided into 5 smaller parts, each with a specific length measured in hunt. The total length of the parts is 130 hunts. Failing to catch a Nightmancer Mouse will extend the length of Night by 3 hunts. An active Mage Tower with the Sunsphere Enchantment will reduce the hunts until the end of Night by 2 after every hunt.

The sub-phases, in order, are:

- Twilight - 35 hunts long

- Midnight - 25 hunts long

- Pitch - 10 hunts long

- Utter Darkness - 25 hunts long

- First Light - 35 hunts long

During Night, the Wall can receive damage under certain circumstances:

- Failure to Catch without mouse pillage (FTC) = 3 damage
- Failure to Catch with mouse pillage (Red box FTC) = 5 damage
- Failure to Attract (FTA) = 5 damage
- Failure to catch Nightfire Mouse = 10 damage
- Encountering Battering Ram Mouse = 15 damage

The damage can be reduced by Moat.

The Hunter can choose to Retreat to Day from this phase.

The Hunter will be transfered to Day if the Wall health drops to 0.

The Hunter will be transfered to Dawn if they progress though all sub-phases with their Wall not completely broken.

#### Dawn

The Hunter can enter this phase by progressing through the Night.

A Damage Multiplier is in effect during this phase. Each catch of the Monster of the Meteor will increase the multiplier by an additional 100%. Damage from encountering Battering Ram Mouse is not multiplied.
The damage can be reduced by Moat, but the reduction applies after the multiplier.

Every FTA deals 5 Damage to your wall.

| Catch | Anger Level | Damage Multiplier | FTC Damage | Red Box FTC Damage |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Irritated | 100% | 3 | 5 |
| 1 | Annoyed | 200% | 6 | 10 |
| 2 | Vexed | 300% | 9 | 15 |
| 3 | Irate | 400% | 12 | 20 |
| 4 | Cross | 500% | 15 | 25 |
| 5 | Angry | 600% | 18 | 30 |
| 6 | Maddened | 700% | 21 | 35 |
| 7 | Ranting | 800% | 24 | 40 |
| 8 | Raving | 900% | 27 | 45 |
| 9 | Raging | 1,000% | 30 | 50 |
| 10 | Fuming | 1,100% | 33 | 55 |
| 11 | Livid | 1,200% | 36 | 60 |
| 12 | Infuriated | 1,300% | 39 | 65 |
| 13 | Seething | 1,400% | 42 | 70 |
| 14 | Frenzied | 1,500% | 45 | 75 |
| 15 | Cheesed Off | 1,600% | 48 | 80 |
| 16-49 | Cheesed Off | Damage multiplier continues to increase by 100%with each Monster of the Meteor catchand damage increases accordingly |  |  |
| 50-98 | totally cheesed off and really annoyed that you would come here and do this and it is absolutely so horrible that you would be so callous and mean and they just hate you so much and will forever remember this moment in time where you offended them so greatly! |  |  |  |
| 99+ | reaching powers of OVER 9000!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! |  |  |  |

The Hunter will be transfered to Day if the Wall health drops to 0.

The Hunter can choose to enter Heart of the Meteor from this phase. Doing so will consume a Fort Rox Portal Projector and add a Ruined Portal Projector to the inventory.

#### Heart of the Meteor

The Hunter can enter this phase from Dawn.

The Hunter's Fort defenses have no effect in this phase. Tower Mana cannot be armed while hunting in this phase.

The Heart of the Meteor's power will be depleted with each attraction, to the point where it is a guaranteed catch on the 10th attraction.

The Hunter can choose to Retreat to Dawn from this phase.

The Hunter will be transfered to Dawn upon catching a Heart of the Meteor.

## Heads-Up Display

### Day, Night, and Dawn HUD

#### Top

- During Day, there is a Defend Fort button, by which the Hunter can click to enter Night phase.

- During Night, the HUD shows the progress of the Night, including:
A progress bar and the number of hunts until the end of Night
A Retreat button which can be clicked to return to Day

- During Dawn, the HUD shows the Damage Multiplier.

#### Middle

The HUD shows the Hunter's Fort. Each part of the Fort can be clicked to access upgrade interface.

- During Dawn, if the Hunter has at least one Fort Rox Portal Projector in inventory, a button appears which can be clicked to enter Heart of the Meteor phase from Dawn.

#### Bottom

The HUD tracks the following items:

- Mage Tower status and Tower Mana
- Inventory of Meteorite Piece, Howlite, and Bloodstone
- An Upgrade button, which can be clicked to access upgrade interface
- Inventory of Gouda, SUPER|brie+, Crescent Cheese, and Moon Cheese

#### Upgrade interface

The HUD shows all the possible upgrades with their cost.

It also shows the inventory of Meteorite Piece, Howlite, Bloodstone, Tower Mana, and Dawn Dust.

### Heart of the Meteor HUD

The center of the HUD is a bar that shows the current Power of the Heart of the Meteor which decreases with each attraction of the mouse: when the bar is depleted, the Heart of the Meteor is a guaranteed catch.

There is also a tracker which shows the Hunter's stock of Sunrise cheese and a button for arming/disarming it.

Hunters have the option to retreat to Dawn from the Heart of the Meteor, with the following confirmation message in the HUD:

## Mice

All mice found in Fort Rox are part of the Meteorite Miners group.

Law, Shadow, and Arcane power type have various degree of effectiveness against them.

### Day Mice

The mice found during Day are part of the Misc. sub-group.

Law traps are very effective against this sub-group, while Shadow and Arcane are less effective.

All mice here are attracted to Standard Cheese as well as Crescent Cheese and Moon Cheese. SUPER|brie+ improves the attraction rate of Mining Materials Manager.

All Day mice drop Meteorite Piece which can be used to repair the Wall and upgrade the Fort.

### Night Mice

The mice found during Night belong to Weremice, Cosmic Critter, and Special sub-groups.

Shadow traps are very effective against Weremice and Nightmancer, while Arcane traps are less effective.
Arcane traps are very effective against Cosmic Critter and Nightfire, while Shadow traps are less effective.
Both Shadow and Arcane traps are less effective against Battering Ram Mouse.

All mice here are attracted to Standard Cheese. Battering Ram Mouse is not attacted to Crescent Cheese and Moon Cheese.

Night mice drop Howlite and Bloodstone which can be used to upgrade the Fort as well as buy needed items from the Shops.

### Heart of the Meteor Mice

The only mouse found in the Heart of the Meteor is the Heart of the Meteor.

Arcane traps are very effective against it, while Shadow traps are less effective.

It is only attracted to Sunrise Cheese.
