## Hunting Strategy

### Adventure Book

Hunters can Capture the Monstrous Black Widow by hunting with Lactrodectus Lancashire Cheese in the Whisker Woods Rift to catch the Monstrous Black Widow Mouse and obtain a Widow's Web.

### Hunting tips by Larry

Just listen to all that noise!

Three distinct groups of mice live in this area, and they're always arguing amongst themselves! Amidst the madness, the Monstrous Black Widow Mouse makes this her home, so if you want to catch her, you'll have to brave the shouting and get sucked in!

As you catch mice from a group in this area, it will enrage mice from that group more and more. As the rage in each group rises, some mice will squeak "I'm telling!" and run to go get larger mice from their group to stand in for them!

The only way to stop this madness is to push a group to the limit, drawing out their biggest, baddest challenger.

Defeat this raging monster, and all of the awestruck mice in the area will run away in fear, forgetting their rage for a while...

You'll also be rewarded with valuable crafting ingredients for your efforts. Keep them safe! You'll need them to lure out the elusive Monstrous Black Widow Mouse!

Sound tricky? It is! Here's a tip: Visit your Crafting Recipe Book and you'll find formulas useful in figuring out which buttons to push. When you have the right cheese and all the mice are angry, she is bound to show up!

Hunt well and hunt bravely! The mice might look really scary, and they really are, but don't be afraid! The worst you can do is get them angry and riled up, right?

| This windmill, and the farm beside it, used to belong to one of Gnawnia's most prosperous farmers before the mice took it over. Windmill mice enjoy the simple life down on the farm, but don't be fooled by their relaxed attitude-- they're laid-back about everything but cheese. |  |

## Overview

Windmill is a hunting location in Gnawnia region.
It requires the rank of Apprentice to access.

## Windmill Mechanic

In this location, a Windmill mechanic is in effect.

- The speed is between 0 and 30 RPM.
When the Hunter first enters the location, it is at 0.
With every catch, the speed increases by 1.
Once the Hunter catch Captain Croissant, the speed becomes 30 RPM immediately.
The speed determines the amount of Packet of Flour dropped by each mouse.

| Speed | Level | Description | Flour drop |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | None | The Windmill is not turning. | 0 |
| 1 - 2 | Ultra low | The Windmill creaks into a slow turn! Keep hunting! | 1 |
| 3 - 8 | Low | The Windmill is turning slowly. Mice now drop a bit more flour! | 2 |
| 9 - 17 | Medium | The Windmill is turning steadily! Mice now drop even more flour! | 3 |
| 18 - 29 | High | The Windmill is turning at high speed. There's flour everywhere! | 4 |
| 30 | Max | The Windmill has been repaired and will remain at top speed! | 5 |

## Heads-Up Display

The HUD shows:

- The current windmill speed and the description of that level
- The Hunter's inventory of Packet of Flour

- The Hunter's inventory of Grilled Cheese

## Mice

All mice found in Windmill are part of Indigenous Mice group.
All power types work against these mice.

All mice here drop Packet of Flour with the amount tied to the windmill speed.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Congratulations, MouseHunter! You've reached the Windmill, a rustic locale where Apprentice MouseHunters go to prove their worth. A group of rowdy country mice overran this farm a while back, and ever since, they've been throwing a hoedown with the farmer's crops!

Recently, a gigantic bread-like mouse has taken up residence inside of the Windmill. This has caused production problems that only a MouseHunter can solve!

• Catch mice in the Windmill
• Every catch helps the Windmill operate more efficiently
• The more you catch, the more the Windmill can produce!
• Collect Packets of Flour and purchase Grilled Cheese
• Capture the Captain Croissant Mouse!

Watch out though! Unlike the Town of Gnawnia, these mice offer more of a challenge to catch, where some may even be able to steal your bait without springing your trap.

So set your trap, MouseHunter, and discover all the wacky and wonderful mouse breeds at the Windmill! Fight hard, and trap often, and you'll be an Initiate in no time!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete the Repair the Windmill adventure in Windmill.

# 📍 Location: Zokor

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Zokor](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Zokor)

| Zokor |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Hollow Heights |  |
| Rank requirement: | Duke/Duchess |
| Map piece: | Labyrinth Key |
| Required Power Type: | ForgottenArcaneShadow |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseGlowing Gruyere |
| Number of Mice: | 38 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral StoreCartographer |
| Release Date: | 9 September 2015 |
| Built upon the ancient ruins of Mousu Picchu, this technologically-advanced superstructure of a city lies behind the endless, twisting corridors of the Labyrinth. Hunters must brave the mind-bending maze before entering this city in order to discover the secrets behind its multi-faceted society. |  |

## Overview

Zokor is a hunting location in Hollow Heights region.

Zokor and Labyrinth are 2 connected hunting locations.
They requires the rank of Duke/Duchess and the Labyrinth Key to access.

The Hunter can enter this location from Labyrinth when certain conditions are met.

Ending a run in this location transferred the Hunter back to Labyrinth. This can be done by either:

- Running out of Stealth
- Catching a Retired Minotaur

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Districts

There are 14 Districts in Zokor.

- The Hunter choose the District they want by picking a Door in the end of the Labyrinth.
- The District type determines the mouse attraction pool and district loot.
- The Alert level determines the Stealth drainage upon failing to catch a mouse.

| District | District type | Alert level | District loot |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Outer Fealty Shrine | Fealty | 1 | Plate of Fealty |
| Inner Fealty Temple | Fealty | 2 | Plate of Fealty |
| Templar's Sanctum | Fealty | 3 | Plate of Fealty |
| Tech Foundry Outskirts | Tech | 1 | Tech Power Core |
| Tech Research Centre | Tech | 2 | Tech Power Core |
| Manaforge | Tech | 3 | Tech Power Core |
| Neophyte Scholar Study | Scholar | 1 | Scholar Scroll |
| Master Scholar Auditorium | Scholar | 2 | Scholar Scroll |
| Dark Library | Scholar | 3 | Scholar Scroll |
| Farming Garden | Farming | 1 | Cavern FungusNightshade |
| Overgrown Farmhouse | Farming | 2 | Cavern FungusNightshade |
| Treasure Room | Treasury | 1 | Plate of FealtyScholar ScrollTech Power Core |
| Treasure Vault | Treasury | 2 | Plate of FealtyScholar ScrollTech Power Core |
| Lair of the Minotaur | Hidden | N/A | N/A |

### Stealth

In 13 Districts (except Lair of the Minotaur), a Stealth mechanic is in effect.

- Upon entering Zokor from Labyrinth, the number of clue of the corresponding District type that the Hunter accumulated becomes Stealth.
If the Hunter chooses Farming Garden with less than 10 Farming clues, then they are given 10 Stealth.
- Hunting in Zokor consumes Stealth.
Catching a mouse drains 1 Stealth.
Failing to attract a mouse drains 1 Stealth.
Failing to catch a mouse drains a number of Stealth equal to the Alert level.
- When the Stealth reaches 0, the Hunter is transferred to Labyrinth.

### Phases

In 3 Districts with Alert level 3, which are Templar's Sanctum, Manaforge, and Dark Library, there are 3 phases:

- Guard
This is the starting phase.
The Hunter is moved to the next phase when 20 mice are caught.
- Leader threat
This phase begins when 20 mice are caught.
During this phase, a District Leader can be attracted.
This phase ends when the District leader is caught.
- Leader beaten
This phase begins when District leader is caught.
During this phase, a District loot is generated every time a mouse is caught.

### Minotaur states

In Lair of the Minotaur:

- A strength meter is used.
The strength is measured in %. It is between 0% and 100%.
When the Hunter enter the Lair, the strength is at 100%.
Each catch decreases the strength by 10%.
Retired Minotaur is easier to catch at lower strength. At 0%, its catch is guaranteed (but its attraction is not).
- There are 4 states corresponding to the strength levels.
The lower the strength, the higher the state, the higher Retired Minotaur attraction rate.
The states are:
Napping: 100% strength
Retired Minotaur cannot be attracted in this state.
Awake: 80% - 90% strength
Enraged: 40% - 70% strength
Reckless: 0% - 30% strength

Catching Retired Minotaur ends the run and transfers the Hunter to the beginning of Labyrinth.

## Heads-Up Display

### District HUD

The HUD shows:

- The district name
- The Shadow Stalker status
- A Retreat button
- The Alert level
- The Stealth

All mice found in this location are part of Citizens of Zokor mouse group.
Forgotten power type is recommended against these mice.

Standard Cheese and Glowing Gruyere can be used here.
The latter removes Shadow Stalker from mouse attraction pool.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Long lost behind the Labyrinth lies the city of Zokor.

Technological advances beyond modern mouseology, mystics and magics that mystify the mind, faculties of oath-sworn fealty, troves of treasure, and a fungal farm more fruitful than the Fungal Cavern! This layered and clustered multiplex metropolis holds many treasures, wonders, secrets and dangers.

Your journey through the Labyrinth has led you into this glorious city. Once the Labyrinth hallways have shifted, the city is put on high alert, as your entrance has now been made known.

The more clues you find while in the maze, the deeper into the districts you can travel, and the longer you can stay there. However, the deeper you go, the more alerted the mice are, and you must remain discrete to avoid capture. These clues give your compass information on the best hiding spots for your district, which is measured in Stealth. The more Stealth you have, the longer you can hunt in the city before being caught by the guards and forced out.

As you hunt within these districts of Zokor, your accumulated Stealth will drop as the guards become more aware of your presence. Every hunt will make your presence more known, and failing to catch a mouse can cause more Stealth to be lost as the mouse reports back to the city guard. Once you run completely out of Stealth, the guards will boot you back out to the beginning of the Labyrinth.

You made it this far, don't let them stop you now!

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete some steps of the Track Down the Retired Minotaur adventure in Zokor.

Stealth Mode - has a 50% chance to not lose Stealth
Heist Mode

Generates additional Plate of Fealty, Tech Power Core, and Scholar Scroll drop from mice in Fealty, Tech, Scholar, and Treasury districts
The quantity generated is equal to the loot from mice.

### Charms

- Glowing Gourd Charm - removes Shadow Stalker from attraction pool

# 📍 Location: Zugzwang's Tower

> **Type**: Location | **Category**: Locations | **Source**: [Zugzwang's Tower](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Zugzwang's_Tower)

Zugzwang's Tower is a Chess themed location.

| Zugzwang's Tower |  |
| --- | --- |
| Region: Rodentia |  |
| Rank requirement: | Lord/Lady |
| Map piece: | Zugzwang's Tower Key |
| Required Power Type: | Tactical |
| Required Cheese: | Standard CheeseCheckmate |
| Number of Mice: | 17 |
| Shops: | Cheese ShoppeTrapsmithCharm ShoppeGeneral Store |
| Release Date: | 26 August 2010 |
| An ordered chaos consumes the enchanted tower as the technological chess mice do battle with their mystical counterparts, all under the auspices of a dark game master who hides in the shadows. Conquer both Kings to find what lurks beyond the chess board, but beware: this is a place of powerful magics, where things are not always what they seem. |  |

## Overview

Zugzwang's Tower is a hunting location in Rodentia region.
It requires the rank of Lord/Lady and the Zugzwang's Tower Key to access.

Mice here become available under certain conditions.

Amplifier modifies power and luck, thus greatly affects the Hunter's ability to catch mice here.

Each trip can be a single or double run. After each run, the Hunter need to charge the amplifier in Seasonal Garden again before doing the next run.

Hunters must complete the Capture the Mystic and Technic Kings Adventure to obtain the Sandtail Call to Arms, which is necessary to gain access to the Fiery Warpath.

Hunters wishing to travel to the Crystal Library location must obtain a Crystal Library Card from the mice found in this location.

## Gameplay Mechanics

### Mouse attraction pool

When the Hunter enters this location, only Mystic Pawn and Technic Pawn are available.
Other mice are added to the attraction pool upon completing certain tasks:

- Catching 8 Pawns of one type adds Knights of the same type to the attraction pool.
- Catching 2 Knights of one type adds Bishops of the same type to the attraction pool.
- Catching 2 Bishops of one type adds Rooks of the same type to the attraction pool.
- Catching 2 Rooks of one type adds Queens of the same type to the attraction pool.
- Catching 1 Queen of one type adds King of the same type to the attraction pool.
- Catching a King of either type adds Chess Master to the attraction pool.
Doing so will also remove all mice of that side from the attraction pool, except:

Be noted that the Hunter needs to use either Checkmate Cheese or Stalemate Charm when catching the said King in order to prevent being transferred to Seasonal Garden and having amplifier reset.

### Tower Amplifier

In Zugzwang's Tower, an Amplifier mechanic is in effect:

- The amplifier acts as a special power and luck multiplier in Zugzwang's Tower.
However, the luck multiplying effect is only active when the amplifier drops below 100%, which means the Hunter won't have more luck than normal even if the amplifier is greater than 100%.
- Catching a Zugzwang's Tower Mouse will deplete the amplifier.
The amount of deplete depends on the mouse, and can be found in the Zugzwang's Tower Mice table.
- Failing to attract a mouse will deplete the amplifier by 3%.
- The amplifier will be reset to 0% and the Hunter transferred to Seasonal Garden when the Hunter either:
Manually retreat from the tower
Catch a Mystic King or Technic King with neither Checkmate Cheese nor Stalemate Charm armed

## Heads-Up Display

In Zugzwang's Tower, the HUD shows:

- The Amplifier bar showing the percentage

- The progress with the Technic and Mystic groups of mice

## Mice

Mice found in Zugzwang's Tower are all members of the Wizard's Pieces mouse group.

Tactical power type is very effective against these mice. There are some Weapons with increased effectiveness against certain sub-groups.
All other types are ineffective.

Mice here are divided into Mystic and Technic sub-group. Within each group, a certain number of lower level mouse must be caught in order to unlock the next level, as stated in Attraction order.

Standard Cheese will attract all mice found in Zugzwang's Tower, except the Chess Master which is only attracted to Checkmate Cheese
Checkmate Cheese will increase the attraction rate of the highest ranked mouse Hunters are currently able to attract.

## Hunting Strategy

### Hunting Tips by Larry

Greetings, Mousehunter!

I hope you've got your Tower Amplifier charged, because the mice of Zugzwang's Tower are going to put up a fight unlike any other.

Long ago, the Tower mice became enchanted chess pieces in a game that never ends. The two teams, Technic and Mystic, use a clear strategy when attacking, much like real chess pieces. Catching eight Pawns releases the Knights. Catching two Knights releases the Bishops. Catching two Bishops releases the Rooks. Catching two Rooks releases the Queen, and catching one Queen releases the King.

Did you get all that? Don't worry if you didn't-- when you enter the tower, your Tower Amplifier also comes equipped with a progress bar to let you know how close you are to the King!

Each time you catch a mouse, or fail to attract, your Tower Amplifier's charge decreases. Once you begin to hunt in the tower, you cannot return to the Seasonal Garden until your Amplifier is drained unless you choose to retreat. Take heed that retreating will reset your Amplifier as well as all of your progress in the tower.

If your strategy is sound, and you capture the King, your progress will be reset and you will be whisked away to the Seasonal Garden. If however, you catch a King using the rare Checkmate Cheese then you shall remain in the tower and a greater adversary will await your challenge.

### Adventure Book

The Hunter can complete steps of the following Adventures in Zugzwang's Tower:

- Capture the Mystic and Technic Kings
- Capture Zurreal the Eternal

### Single and Double Runs

The primary goal of each tower run is to catch a King.

In a Single Run, the Hunter attempts to catch only either Mystic King or Technic King.

- Catching a King without Checkmate Cheese or Stalemate Charm armed will reset the Amplifier and transfer the Hunter to Seasonal Garden.
- Hunters who wish to stay in Zugzwang's Tower after catching a King will need to have either Checkmate Cheese armed or any Standard Cheese with Stalemate Charm armed when catching the first King.

In a Double Run, the Hunter attempts to catch both Kings.

- The Hunter needs to catch the first King with either Checkmate Cheese armed or any Standard Cheese with Stalemate Charm armed to stay in Zugzwang's Tower.
- The other King can then be unlocked and attracted as normal.
- Hunters who wish to stay in Zugzwang's Tower after catching the second King will need to have either Checkmate Cheese armed or any Standard Cheese with Stalemate Charm armed when catching the second King.

## Location-Specific Effects

### Weapons

- Mystic Pawn Pincher:
Increases attraction of Mystic Pawn
Decreases attraction of Technic Pawn
Has a bonus of 10,920 Power and 51 Luck against Mystic Pawn
Has a penalty of 60 Power and 0.05 Luck against Technic Pawn
- Technic Pawn Pincher:
Increases attraction of Technic Pawn
Decreases attraction of Mystic Pawn
Has a bonus of 10,920 Power and 51 Luck against Technic Pawn
Has a penalty of 60 Power and 0.05 Luck against Mystic Pawn
- Blackstone Pass Trap:
Has a bonus of 1,800 Power and 6 Luck against Mystic mice
Has a penalty of 2,400 Power and 9 Luck against Technic mice
- Obvious Ambush Trap:
Has a bonus of 1,800 Power and 6 Luck against Technic mice
Has a penalty of 2,400 Power and 9 Luck against Mystic mice
- Zugzwang's Ultimate Move - has a chance to instantly catch a mouse if the Tower Amplifier is not at 0%

### Charms

- Uncharged Scholar Charm - adds a Scholar Charm to the Hunter's inventory upon catching a Chess Master
- Rook Crumble Charm - has a bonus of 300% power bonus against Mystic Rook and Technic Rook
- Stalemate Charm - prevents Amplifier reset and allows the Hunter to remain in Zugzwang's Tower upon catching a Mystic King, Technic King, or Chess Master
